The two halves together are a complete wall.** **The second is the ordering itself: a joint year bootstrap (it must be joint — the eight items ask the same people in the same years), 28 pairs, family-wide BH, 16/28 distinguishable in the main cell and 16–18 across all eight specification cells. And once those 16 pairs are listed, the pattern is not a gradient but three clumps: 0/8 distinguishable within clumps, 16/20 between.** ⇒ **One sentence about people: the devout are not on a continuous axis of resistance — they are doing three different things across three groups of questions. On the top two they changed *more* than everyone else: whether schools should teach sex (2.19) and whether there should be laws against racial intermarriage (1.84). On the middle four they changed, but only about four-tenths as much: homosexuality, teen sex, suicide when ruined, hitting children. On the bottom two they barely moved at all: prayer in schools (0.13) and whether government should aid Black people (0.11). And the three clumps are not "sexual versus non-sexual": each clump contains both.** ⚠ The partition was read off the same data, so those two counts are **description, not test**; testing it needs held-out items this instrument cannot supply.⚠⚠ **the "three clumps" in this paragraph are retracted in `#797`**: the two bottom items' intervals contain both 0 and the middle's 0.41 and cannot be placed; what the data supports is **one cleanly separated pair plus six items that cannot be sorted into levels**. ⚠ The half of this paragraph that stands is the `0/8` within-clump result — evidence that **the top pair separates from everything else**, **not** evidence of a three-level partition; split out in `#797`, middle/bottom resolves only 4/8.` ⚠ **and since `#806` this must carry one more sentence: the "common latent shift" control is **out of this design's reach** for the top pair — both calibration arms exceed the 30% reachable-range limit (`sexeduc` 42%/31%, `racmar` 72%/71%) ⇒ zero usable cells, **structurally impossible, not a debt**.** [#791「不是一条轴,是三堆;而解释它这条路在算术上就是关着的」]`。 ⚠⚠ **Before reorganising the page around the new cut, the next round went to count how many lines still rest on the old one — and the answer is that the sieve itself failed, in the most expensive way this methodology knows.** The sieve hit 40 lines, and two positive controls were run: **the first asks "can it see?" and passed (it found the lines that overturn the cut); the second asks "is what it sees the thing I am about to claim about?" and failed — it could not find the one line I already know is a USE.** The reason is concrete: the vocabulary expects "sexual morality" followed by "a/special", while that line says "a law about sexual **morality**". ⇒ **The shared blind spot is nameable here: the sieve looks for lines that DISCUSS the cut — those write "non-sexual" as a word — while I need lines that USE it, and those only say "sexual morality". The two use opposite language, so the sieve's recall is zero on exactly the class I need.** ⇒ **Neither the 40 hits nor the 0 USEs may be used to decide a reorganisation.** ⚠ And the confound control written before the run saved the round: **USE and DISCUSS must be separated** — otherwise the lines that overturn the cut get counted as lines resting on it, **a repair read as an epidemic, and a whole page reorganised on it.**`[#792「正对照过了,而筛子对我要主张的那个东西是瞎的」]`。 ⚠⚠ **After that UNVERIFIED, the next round changed route: stop defining the population by vocabulary, define it structurally.** Every row in the main table ends with `(Entry N)` — a population that is mechanically enumerable and independent of whatever words I happen to use, **so recall is 1 by construction.** All 91 claims were read (the criterion — replace "sexual" with "moral" and ask whether the claim loses its content — fixed in writing before any row was read; and because I knew I wanted "no reorganisation", both a strict and a lenient count were reported). **Strict 9, lenient 12, against a threshold of 10 — the decision straddles the threshold.** ⚠⚠ **And it was exactly "the decision depends on me" that forced the check, at which point the criterion turned out to be wrong: the substitution test asks whether a row *uses* the cut, while the decision asks whether its premise has *fallen*.** What the previous row overturned is one specific estimand — the devout/non-devout rate-of-change ratio on GSS — **and it did not overturn education effects, domain cohesion or facet structure, most of which live on other instruments.** ⇒ **Exactly one row is affected, the one that already carries its forward pointer.** ⇒ **One sentence about this work itself: this is the third time in this session that an adjacent quantity was substituted for the one being judged — counting pairs where the question was structural, a positive control sharing the sieve's blind spot, and a criterion asking "used" where the decision asked "fallen". None of the three was an arithmetic error.** ⚠ And the agreement with the previous round's guess must be stated precisely: **that round guessed right using a sieve with zero recall; this one settled it by enumerating all 91 claims — guessing right is not settling, and that is exactly why the earlier UNVERIFIED was correct.**`[#793「结构定总体之后,受影响的只有一条;而判据又错了一次,同一族第三次」]`。 ⚠⚠ **And underneath those three clumps sits a design choice that has been in every round since `#776` and that the ledger never once named: "the most devout third" is defined per year.** America secularised over fifty years, so the line itself drifts downward ⇒ **part of "the devout changed their minds" might not be minds changing at all, but who counts as devout.** `#779` pinned birth cohort, which **cannot pin this line**. ⇒ First, measure it: `REL` is z-scored on the pooled sample, so the ruler is absolute — **the line fell 0.302 standard deviations end to end, with a range of 0.458 — it genuinely moved, and by more than my pre-registered 0.25 threshold.** ⇒ Then pin it to **1974's line** and recompute all eight items: **not one of the eight moved beyond its own bootstrap width, and the three clumps survive intact.** ⇒ **One sentence about people: the most devout third of America did become less devout over fifty years — but that is not where those ratios came from. Pin "who counts as devout" to a half-century-old standard and all eight ratios stay inside their own error. These are not the same people only by relative rank — this was minds changing, not a change of membership.** ⚠ And this verdict is the **third outcome** I wrote into the prediction matrix before running, stronger than "the line held still": **not that it didn't move, but that its moving doesn't matter.** ⚠⚠ The first version was killed on the spot by its own positive control: `pd.qcut` bins are right-closed so the top bin is `> q67`, and I had written `>= q67` — **`REL` is a mean of three ordinal z-scores and the boundary is thick with ties**, so tied respondents landed in different bins under the two implementations. Once fixed, the difference is exactly 0.⚠ **how "the three clumps survive intact" in this paragraph must be read after `#797`**: this paragraph is **not wrong** — it established that **the partition is invariant to the stratification line's drift**, which remains true; what changed is that **the partition itself is unresolvable**. **A partition that cannot be resolved staying stable is not evidence that the partition exists.**`[#794「那条线真的漂了,而它没有制造那些比值」]`。 ⚠⚠ **The library defect the previous row caught in passing was fixed next, then backtested — and the backtest deflated the previous row's own phrasing.** The fix keys on a **mechanical rule rather than my judgement: a method whose name contains `control` is a control** — because "is this a control or a kill?" is exactly the family this session has already got wrong three times; and it wraps once at the instance rather than editing thirty-odd call sites, **because editing call sites means missing one, and the one missed is the next false retraction.** ⇒ Then the backtest: **4 candidate rounds, 0 occurrences.** The five ledger entries that quote the library's OVERTURNED **all use the method that registers correctly**, and all four candidates recorded UNVERIFIED. ⇒ **One sentence about this toolchain: no false retraction ever reached the ledger, and it was not the library that prevented it — it was the unwritten convention that every round computes its own verdict and treats the library's line as advisory. An unwritten safety net is one refactor from vanishing.** ⚠ And one piece of the previous row's wording is withdrawn: leading with "369 exposed" reads as a risk magnitude — **the right order is 4 candidates, 0 occurrences, 369 exposed, and the number to lead with is the zero. Not a miscount, but which number went at the front of the sentence.**`[#795「缺陷是真的,而它一次都没到过账本:救我的是一条从没写下来的惯例」]`。 ⚠⚠ **And the three clumps, the axis, the ceiling, the robustness in the line above — all of it had only ever used one estimator: take each stratum's yearly mean, regress on year, divide. That discards every bit of variation inside a year.** The specification curve had swept bootstrap draws, and q, and the definition of the difference — **three post-hoc statistical knobs, and not one of them "how is this ratio estimated".** ⇒ Switching to an individual-level interaction, computed one person at a time: **all eight ratios fall inside the original year-clustered interval and not one clump member changes.** ⇒ **One sentence about people: the three clumps are not an artifact of how I summarised the years. Weight every respondent equally instead of every year equally and not a word of the conclusion changes — which of these things the devout moved on and which they did not is about them, not about my binding them into one point per year.** ⚠⚠ **And the thing most worth recording is that my three estimators were two**: the third arm (individual level, weighted 1/n within year-stratum) is **item-by-item identical** to the first, max difference 9.3e−13 — **"individual-level regression with 1/n weights" simply *is* the year-mean regression.** I built it to separate "does discarding individuals matter" from "year-equal versus person-equal weighting", **and in this design those are not two things at all.** An arm that could not have come out otherwise is not a measurement — labelled a derivation.⚠ **likewise, "not one clump member changes" here is, after `#797`, the stability of an unresolvable partition** — this paragraph's conclusions about the **estimator** (8/8 inside the interval, the third arm being an identity) are **entirely unaffected**; only the name of the object being tested is.`[#796「三堆不是逐年取均值取出来的;而我那三个估计量其实是两个」]`。 ⚠⚠ **Two consecutive rounds confirming the clumps' robustness is a basin, so the next round deliberately hit the softest part of the page's centre — and it collapsed.** The starting point is a correction of my own writing: `#791`'s "16/20 between-pairs distinguishable" pooled three different boundaries. Split out: **top/middle 8/8, top/bottom 4/4, and middle/bottom only 4/8** — `suicide2` and `teensex` separate from neither bottom item. ⇒ Then the two questions the page had been reading as one. **First, rule out "unaskable": all eight denominators clear their own permutation null (1.82–2.50×), so on school prayer and government aid to Black people society genuinely did move.** **Then the bottom intervals: `prayer` [−0.232, +0.436] and `helpblk` [−0.427, +0.628] — containing both 0 and the middle's 0.41.** ⇒ **One sentence about people, and it retracts my own previous one: on these two questions the devout's rate of change relative to everyone else is measured too imprecisely to be told apart either from zero or from the middle group. "The devout barely moved on these" was never established — what is established is that we cannot say.** ⇒ **"Three clumps" is retracted. The shape the data actually supports is: one cleanly separated pair (sex education 2.19, laws against racial intermarriage 1.84, both excluding 0.41 and 0) plus six items that cannot be sorted into levels.** ⚠ And `#791`'s intervals disagree with this round's, which must not be blamed on the seed: the two rounds used different bootstraps (joint versus per-item) — **the difference contains both estimator and seed, I cannot separate them, and I will not guess — and a conclusion that flips under two equally defensible bootstraps is itself the evidence.**`[#797「底堆归不了堆:三堆撤回,剩下「干净的一对 + 排不出层的六题」」]`。 ⚠⚠ **The line above retracted the three clumps, and nine places on the page were still using them — a retraction that lands only in the ledger has not happened.** Enumerating them structurally (96 rows carrying `(Entry N)` plus the segments the inline anchors cut, recall 1 by construction) and correcting each. ⇒ **And the criterion has a branch whose omission would have destroyed two rounds of good work:** `#791` **asserts** the three-level partition and is retracted outright; but "the line drifted and the ratios did not" and "changing the estimator changes nothing" are **not wrong** — they established that **the partition is invariant under those perturbations**, which still holds, and what changed is that **the partition itself cannot be resolved**. ⇒ **A partition that cannot be resolved staying stable is not evidence that it exists — that is an annotation, not a retraction.** ⚠⚠ And the verifier's first version reported 15 still bare, which was **its own unit being wrong**: attribution used the nearest preceding inline anchor, but table rows have none, so all 96 were attributed to a single anchor; and the pointer window was measured in **characters**, while English paragraphs run about twice the Chinese — **the same annotation received opposite verdicts on the two pages.** With the unit defined structurally, zero remain bare.`[#798「撤回落地了,而更正分两种:断言它 vs 证明它稳定」]`。 ⚠⚠ **With the three clumps retracted, only the top pair was still standing — so the next round went after it, and the reason was written in the data: `sexeduc` has the smallest denominator of the eight (0.00128) and the largest ratio (2.192).** A ratio of two near-zero slopes is Cauchy-like, and instability is denominator-driven — **so was the top pair just a small denominator amplifying noise into r>1?** ⚠ The naive version of that suspicion was already half-refuted by the data: the 2nd and 3rd smallest denominators carry 0.410 and 0.126, **not large at all**, so ranking cannot settle it ⇒ **build the world**: keep all eight **real denominators** and pin the true ratio at 0.45 for every item. ⇒ **In that world the top pair produces r>1.5 with frequency 0.0005 and 0.0000 (0.0043 and 0.0000 in the other arm) against a threshold of 0.10; their observed values are 2.192 and 1.841.** ⇒ **One sentence about people, and it is the only one on this page to have survived a level-4 attack: the most devout third of Americans changed more than everyone else — roughly twice as much — on whether schools should teach sex and whether there should be laws against racial intermarriage, and that is not a small denominator amplifying noise.** ⚠⚠ And the σ=0 control gave something worth more than a pass: **with no noise, a small denominator produces no inflation whatsoever** (all eight return exactly 0.450000). ⇒ **What inflates a ratio is noise; the small denominator is only its amplifier** — so "instability is denominator-driven" must be read as *the interval widens*, **not as *the point estimate is pushed up*; two different mechanisms I had been treating as one.**` ⚠ **and since `#806` this must carry one more sentence: the "common latent shift" control is **out of this design's reach** for the top pair — both calibration arms exceed the 30% reachable-range limit (`sexeduc` 42%/31%, `racmar` 72%/71%) ⇒ zero usable cells, **structurally impossible, not a debt**.** [#799「顶对经住了造世界那一级:小分母 + 噪声造不出 2.19 与 1.84」]`。 ⚠⚠ **Inside the previous line's "stop looking for candidate explanations" was something it had not finished saying — an asymmetry.** Confirming a candidate at n=8 needs |ρ|≥0.90 and that door is shut; **but refuting one needs only a counterexample placing top and bottom on the same side, and counterexamples do not consume power.** ⇒ So all six candidates decidable from the question stems alone were listed at once, with the criterion fixed before any result: **is the positive class exactly those two items? Result: 0 of 6 survive.** "Should the state legislate or run schools" dies on `prayer` and `helpblk` — all four ask whether the state should act, and the latter two sit at the bottom; "about minors" dies on `spanking` and `teensex`; "race-related" dies on `helpblk`; "sex-related" dies on `homosex` and `teensex`. ⇒ **One sentence about this work: what makes those two items special is not state involvement, not race, not children, and not sex — the six handiest explanations are all blocked by counterexamples inside the same set of question stems.** ⚠⚠ And what must be said in the same breath: **this route can only subtract.** There are 28 two-element subsets of eight items, so hitting a given pair at random is 1/28; across six candidates **the chance that at least one hits by luck is 0.196 — about one in five** ⇒ **its "no" is hard and its "yes" is free**, so this round reports only what died and treats no survivor as support.`[#800「MDE 关的是确认,不是反驳:六个候选全部被反例杀掉,一个统计量都没用」]`。 ⚠⚠ **The page's loudest sentence — that the devout changed more on the top pair — is a ratio, and a ratio cannot distinguish three stories: catching up, keeping pace, overtaking. The page had never reported the four strata's start and end levels.** And something harder: the ceiling control built earlier had in fact only run on four *other* items — **the one pair still standing here is exactly the place it never covered.** ⇒ So report the levels first; they are direct observation with no model: **on sex education the devout go 1.303→1.176 while the non-devout go 1.110→1.075 (scale 1–3), the gap closing 0.193→0.101; on laws against racial intermarriage the devout go 1.550→1.873 against 1.724→1.919 (scale 1–2), the gap closing −0.174→−0.047.** ⇒ **One sentence about people, and it is the half the page was missing: they did not move out ahead of everyone else — they started further away and closed most of the gap. What "changed more" looks like in levels is catching up.** ⚠⚠ And the model half is UNVERIFIED because the negative control fired against expectation — **and how it fired is what is most worth recording**: I pre-registered "the further the starting line, the more room to move", and measured **[1.0, 0.712, 0.498, 0.343] — monotonically smaller**. ⇒ **Observed change is produced by mass crossing thresholds, and mass far from a threshold does not cross — the floor compresses exactly as the ceiling does. "They started further away so they had more room" is false in this model.** Pre-registration honoured: UNVERIFIED, the control is not retro-fitted, and neither item's forced value may be read.`[#801「两题都是「追上」;而地板和天花板一样压缩,我的镜像直觉是反的」]`. ⚠ **And `premarsx`, the one item not at its ceiling, did not rescue that direction either.** Across three statistics and two cuts the six cells disagree: at terciles raw rises, the slope rises, the **marginal-matched ρ falls**; at quintiles raw **falls**, the slope rises, matched falls. And the raw ρ's spread is only **0.8×** its own Gaussian null (0.75× at quintiles), **indistinguishable from it**; the censoring gate also fails (top stratum endpoint **60.9%**). ⇒ **The verdict is UNVERIFIED; both gates were written before the run, and no threshold was changed and no side taken.** The only statistic monotone under both cuts is the **marginal-matched ρ, and it falls** ⇒ **the "runs the other way" flagged in the row above is not a finding.** ⚠ And the three statistics are **immune to different things** (raw eats sd(Y), the slope eats censoring, matching flattens Y's distribution) — **so where they disagree is exactly where "what is this interaction defined on" remains unsettled.** ⚠⚠ **A second consecutive UNVERIFIED on the same question means change direction rather than chase a third, and the interaction line is suspended here.** ⇒ **The one sentence about people this round supports is about the instrument's reach: among the most devout Americans, not one of GSS's four sexual-attitude items can read a strength of relation — even premarital sex, the only unsaturated one, has 60.9% piled on the endpoint in the top quintile.**` ⚠ **and since `#806` this must carry one more sentence: the "common latent shift" control is **out of this design's reach** for the top pair — both calibration arms exceed the 30% reachable-range limit (`sexeduc` 42%/31%, `racmar` 72%/71%) ⇒ zero usable cells, **structurally impossible, not a debt**.** [#773「连唯一没饱和的那一题也判不了,而这是第二次 ⇒ 换方向」]`. ⚠ And testing that boundary on NSFG returned **an instrument mismatch** rather than a failure to replicate: GSS's item runs "always wrong … not wrong at all", where **the strict pole is the devout's modal answer (87.8%)**; NSFG's `sxok18` is an **agree/disagree** scale where **the strict pole is rare for everyone (0.8% overall, 1.2% in the most-attending stratum)** — **endpoint share is not the same statistic across the two formats.** ⇒ **The boundary stays scoped to GSS and is not promoted to "items of this kind".** ⚠ And that arm first caught three real errors of mine (dropping `attndnow`'s largest code 7, reversing the code order, and the rarity of the strict pole), **all stopped by its own stratification control** — the one time this round's controls actively saved me. ⚠⚠ **And that sentence was withdrawn by a second instrument inside the same round.** MFQ carries religious attendance, `chastity` and authority, so no exemption was available: **on MFQ the more devout stratum shows a *higher* ρ(chastity, authority) (+0.3865 → +0.5211)**, the exact opposite of GSS. ⚠⚠ **And the assumption they differ on is sitting in the per-stratum sd column**: **GSS's decline comes with a 37% variance collapse (0.856→0.542) while MFQ's rise comes with flat variance (0.783→0.838)** — **the most devout agree with one another about extramarital sex, so there is less variance left to correlate and the correlation is mechanically depressed.** ⇒ **GSS's direction is most likely that artefact.** ⇒ **The claim that devotion weakens the tie is withdrawn; and I had already written the limitation that overturns it (the per-stratum sd) in the same round while still resting the conclusion on direction — "the magnitude is unreadable" and "the direction is unreadable" are two different things.** **What survives: the interaction is not a function of second moments, and both instruments exceed their own Gaussian nulls (1.08×–2.60× and 1.93×–2.93×).**

**And `#801` immediately exposed something larger: this page has published `r` for eight items and had never once reported any item's gap between the two strata.** `r` says **who moved faster**; the gap says **whether they ended up closer together or further apart** — and **the same `r` is compatible with two opposite stories about people**. Across the eight items (devout − non-devout, both specifications agreeing 8/8): **3 narrowed, 5 widened**. **And on homosexuality — the item this whole project has centred on — the divide did not close, it roughly tripled: the devout stood 0.55 points from everyone else in 1974 and 1.65 points away in 2024, on a 3-point span. They did change their minds (`r = 0.41`); everyone else changed far faster, so the distance grew.** ⇒ **in this data `r<1` and "the divide is growing" are the same fact** (`homosex`, `teensex`, `spanking` alike), **while the page had only ever read `r` as "how much they changed their minds", never as the rate of polarisation it also is.** ⚠ The three that narrowed are exactly the two where the devout moved fastest (`sexeduc`, `racmar`) plus `helpblk` — and `helpblk` **crossed zero**: on whether government should help Black Americans, the devout went from below everyone else to above them. ⚠ But `helpblk`'s `r` interval spans both 0 and 0.41 (`#797`) ⇒ **the ratio side is unreadable while the gap side is direct observation; these do not contradict each other, they are different quantities.** ⚠⚠ **One thing this round explicitly refuses to do, with the reason written down: no regression of `Δgap` on `gap0`.** `Δgap` contains `gap0`, so that is Oldham 1962's conditioning on the outcome, **which necessarily yields a negative slope — algebra, not a finding** ⇒ **"the starting point decides whether a gap closes" is untestable in this design, registered as impossible, and nobody may run that regression on this round's numbers.** ⚠ **These gaps carry no interval**, recorded honestly as a debt against `#783`. `[#802「五条鸿沟在裂,三条在合」]`

**And `#802`'s sentence immediately owed a control, so the next round went and built it — deliberately choosing a direction whose positive outcome would be unwelcome: if it fired, the headline died.** The question: **does a common latent shift — both strata changing their minds identically — already produce a widening gap when read from two different starting points on a 1–4 ordinal scale?** The pre-registration says no (median `explained` across eight cells = +0.385 < 0.50 ⇒ real divergence), with all four controls passing. **⚠⚠ But reading those eight cells after the verdict, four of them carry the defect I had written into the ledger one round earlier — I built it again**: `fit` calibration on the non-devout arm demands **89.8% of the reachable range**, where `#801` had just established "no control may demand a change near the reachable limit" and set the standard at 30%; **the two cells that dragged the median below 0.50 are exactly those.** The four internally consistent cells are +0.403, +0.429, +0.527, +0.557 — **median +0.478, astride the threshold.** ⇒ **The substantive verdict is "this design cannot separate them", and it carries more content than either pole:** **on homosexuality, roughly half of the gap that opened between the devout and everyone else is "the same change, read from different starting points" —** **meaning that even if the two groups had changed by exactly the same amount inwardly, their survey answers would still have drifted apart; only the other half is the two groups genuinely moving differently.** **So "the religious divide is widening" is true, but gentler than it sounds: half of it is one shared shift projected onto a coarse ruler, not two groups walking in opposite directions.** ⚠ The pre-registered verdict is still honoured to the letter and reported as such, per `#787` — **naming a defect in the criterion afterwards licenses no post-hoc change of criterion.** `[#803「预注册说「真分歧」」]`
⚠ **This number tracks sample size, not how true the claim is**: measured, `corr(ratio, n) = +0.8829` while `corr(ratio, effect) = −0.3929` — **the largest effects have the lowest ratios** (they are the n≈75–97 society-side ones). **Do not read 18.46 as "most reliable"; it is merely the biggest sample** (Entry 706 / `#706`).


**〔…〕** = **the instrument this claim routes through**. ⚠ *Cross-instrument* has two readings: **broad** (the entry mentions ≥2) gives 9–12, while **strict** (actually measured on ≥2) gives only **4** — `Entry 653 · 686 · 689 · 690`. **Both are reported; do not trust the broad one alone** (Entry 704). ⚠ **The count is still 4, but `Entry 653`'s two instruments changed identity**: it rested on GSS + NSFG, and the NSFG leg falls inside its own null under optimal signs (Entry 718), so it now rests on **GSS + MFQ**. **A count holding still does not mean what sits under it held still.**

**Person side** (GSS · NSFG · MFQ)

⇒ replaced by the version that *is* identified (**stratify, do not adjust**: among people of similar age, does religiosity still split the nineties) ⇒ **second wall: the power gate stopped it before any result was read — the three bands' "noise half-width ÷ amount to be resolved" are 0.86×, 0.72× and 1.80× against a pre-registered requirement of <0.5, and none passes** | ⚠⚠⚠ **and the positive control said the same thing independently, which is this round's most transferable point**: a departure of **3× that band's own full-span movement** planted into one band's 1990s **did not survive** the family-wide BH ⇒ **the power gate says "the noise exceeds what must be resolved" and the positive control says "even a planted giant cannot be recovered" — two independent signals pointing at one wall**; ⚠ and `realstat` requires separating "the design lacks power" from "the control is malformed": **this round cannot apportion them**, but the two signals agree and the power gate was computed independently of the control ⇒ **"no power" is the simpler explanation, and that is an explanation, not a proof**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, and this round it is about what cannot be asked: we can see that the religious rift and the age rift both opened in the nineties, but we cannot say whether they are the same rift — and not because nobody looked, but because the question has two walls: the "remove the age effect" arithmetic it naturally invites is not identified at all, and the version that is identified needs more people per age band than fifty years of this survey provide** `[#835「两层墙」]` (Entry 835) 〔GSS〕 ⟨⚠⚠ **this round's methodological gain is the gate's position, not its content**: the pattern of the last twenty rounds was "run → read → find it inadmissible → retract"; **here power was written as gate ⓪, ahead of every judgement about people ⇒ the result was stopped before reading rather than withdrawn after** — **`#822` taught "measure whether the change helps before rebuilding on it"; this round moves that same lesson forward into a gate instead of another after-the-fact honesty**; negative control: 60 uniform worlds ⇒ "at least one survivor" **0.083 ± 0.036** against a threshold of 0.121 ⇒ passes (⚠ **the expectation is ≤ q, not 0**); ⚠ the pre-written confound: **the within-band religiosity tercile cutpoints differ by band** (the oldest band's least-devout third may be more devout than the youngest band's most-devout third) ⇒ printed per band, **and the stratification is done within band only**; the mismatch cannot be fixed, only measured; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: the three-band cut is mine and **another cut gives another power** — but coarser bands weaken the "similar age" premise itself, **which is this design's dilemma, not a tunable parameter**⟩|
| **Switch the unit to the society: the "condemnation ↔ rarity" association is visible only to one pair of coders** | **All 16 prior rounds this session used GSS, one instrument, one unit (measured from `git log`) ⇒ `§3`'s basin criterion is binding** ⇒ change the instrument (GSS → **SCCS, 186 societies**) and the unit (person/decade → **society**), and deliberately build the design so that **the outcome I would find unwelcome is the easiest one to obtain**; positive control ρ=−0.912, p=0.00025; **⚠⚠ one point against myself: the pre-registration used two yardsticks in one criterion and wrote down only one** — by MDE the verdict is C, by the permutation p the only arm that moves is the same-coder one (**missing BH's rank-2 threshold of 0.0333 by 0.0002**) ⇒ **two yardsticks give worlds C and B; the verdict follows the pre-registration (C), B's evidence is written down honestly, and no verdict is changed after the fact (swapping yardsticks post-run is what `#111` forbids)**, registered as `#836`①; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: **the outcome side is single-instrument permanently** — SCCS carries only Broude & Greene's coding of homosexual frequency, so a second instrument is **结构性地拿不到**; and worse: **if condemnation drove the behaviour into hiding, the ethnographer recorded "absent" — condemnation causally manufactures the appearance of rarity, and that is not a controllable confound, it is the measurement itself**; `societies.csv` has **no language-family column** ⇒ Galton's problem could only be addressed with geographic blocks cut explicitly from Lat/Long, **a substitute, not an equivalent**⟩|
| **Do the three rulers agree? | **The nineties rift opened because the secular side left — not because the devout side dug in** | **`§0.2`: `#835` two walls · `#836` overturned · `#837` UNVERIFIED — three consecutive rounds whose product was "cannot", and if honesty were the objective function then switching me off would be its maximum** ⇒ **this round's goal was to make something stand**; **measured with `grep`: in 823 ledger entries not one round had ever looked at the two strata's own trajectories — eight hundred rounds measured the GAP, and none asked which side was moving when it opened**; ⚠⚠ **and before measuring anything, one piece of arithmetic must be labelled: `dep(devout) − dep(secular) ≡ dep(gap)` is an identity, not a finding (the gap IS the difference of the strata, and the references are linear too) ⇒ the sum is an identity; the split is the measurement** | **`homosex`, 28 qualifying years (1974–2024, ≥120 per stratum per year), n=24,264; full 50-year movement: devout +0.5958, secular +1.3183**; **the nineties: devout +0.1137 [−0.016,+0.238] contains zero · secular +0.6266 [+0.434,+0.818] excludes zero ⇒ the secular stratum carries 85% of the two strata's total departure**; **`G4`: all decades × both strata, 10 cells published whole including the disagreeing ones ⇒ BH leaves 5, BY leaves 4**; ⚠ **the pre-written ceiling confound is genuinely ruled out here: the devout stratum moved 1.26 → 1.47 in the nineties with 2.74 of headroom still above it, using only 8% — it had ample room to move and did not depart**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, and this time it stands: the rift of the nineties opened not because religious people became less tolerant, but because non-religious people moved faster than they themselves had for the previous twenty years; the rift was pulled open by one side accelerating while the other kept roughly to its own pace. And the religious side departed from its own pace exactly once in fifty years — in the 2010s, in the direction of greater permissiveness** ⚠⚠ **`#846` narrows the scope in place**: this holds **only on `homosex`**. Across eight items × two strata, the secular stratum departs on **1/8** (only `homosex`) and the devout on **1/8** (only `sexeduc`); over the 16-cell family BH and BY each leave one, and the ceiling flag fires **0/16** ⇒ **"the non-religious moved faster" is not a sentence about those people, it is a sentence about this item.** `[#838「是世俗那边走了」]` (Entry 838) 〔GSS〕 ⟨**⚠⚠⚠ this round's methodological gain became visible only after the positive control failed twice, and it transfers further than the result does**: ① the expectation was written as `A×(1 − decade span/full span)`, but the plant sat on an **interior year of the decade** ⇒ the full-span movement is untouched ⇒ the reference is unchanged ⇒ the departure shifts by exactly A (**the instrument was right and my expectation was wrong**, `#818`'s class); ② replanting on `S[-1]=2024` and predicting a net rise in the 2010s — **except 2024 is not in the 2010s cell at all** (`2024//10*10 = 2020`, and the 2020s were dropped for having fewer than 3 qualifying years) ⇒ **the last five years belong to no decade cell**; ③ **the shared root: I kept deriving the expectation from my picture of the bucketing rather than from the bucketing the code computes** ⇒ **the fix is not to hand-derive another scalar but to write the expectation as a formula read off the code and let it predict the entire grid at once**: `Δdeparture = A·[yp=d_last] − A·[yp=d_first] − (A·[yp=S_last] − A·[yp=S_first])·d_span/span` ⇒ **4 plant sites × 5 decades = 20 predictions, 0 mismatches, cross-leak 0.00e+00** — **a hand-derived scalar validates one cell; a formula read off the code validates the whole grid, and what it validates is that I read the code rather than that I remembered it**; negative control: a world where both strata move strictly uniformly ⇒ departures **−7.4e−16 / +2.8e−17** (⚠ **this zero should be zero**: a uniform world has no departure by definition); ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: ① "devout/secular" is a **year-by-year tercile**, a relative position and **not a fixed threshold of belief** — as a whole society becomes less devout the same tercile holds different people (`#835` already showed the entanglement with age is structurally non-identified, and this round does not reopen it); ② **the 2020s cell was dropped for having fewer than 3 qualifying years ⇒ the most recent five years enter no conclusion here**, registered rather than "planned"⟩|
| **A ruler with no upper end: `#838` holds in 10 of 10 cells, and the ceiling biased the opposite way from the one I feared** | **`§3` basin: `#838`'s "first pulled open, then caught up" is a pleasing story, and pleasing is exactly why it had to be checked** ⇒ **the threat was printed in `#838`'s own headroom table: by the 2010s the secular stratum had reached 3.08→3.49 on a scale ending at 4, leaving 0.92 of room and using 44% of it — for a stratum pressed against the ceiling, "slowing down" may not be slowing at all, only the ruler running out** ⇒ **change the estimator, not the instrument**: treat the 1–4 responses as a latent continuum cut by three thresholds (ordered probit, **thresholds estimated once on the full sample and held fixed, variance fixed at 1, one parameter μ per cell**) ⇒ **μ is unbounded by the 1–4 edges**, and recompute **the whole grid** rather than only the threatened cell | **10 of 10 cells agree with `#838`**; 1990s secular latent **+0.5436 [+0.372,+0.725] excludes zero** (mean scale +0.6266) · 2010s devout latent **+0.3440 [+0.167,+0.545] excludes zero** (mean scale +0.3331) ⇒ **BH leaves 5, BY leaves 5 (`#838` left 4)**; ⚠⚠ **and the most transferable result is that the ceiling biased in the opposite direction from the one I feared** — the 2010s secular cell is **+0.2586** on the latent scale against **+0.1947** on the mean scale, so **the ceiling had been suppressing the secular cell, biasing toward exactly the "the devout caught up" story I liked; correcting it makes the convergence weaker, not stronger**, with the gap-level 2010s departure falling from **+0.1384 to +0.0854 (−38%)** ⇒ **a confound can be named correctly and still point the opposite way, and only measuring it tells you which**; ⇒ **a sentence about people: the rift was first pulled open by the non-religious, and later the religious moved for the first time too — and neither of those is an artifact of the ruler running out. ⚠ **`#846`'s scope applies here too**: this round also ran **only `homosex`**. Across eight items the secular stratum departs on **1/8** and the devout on **1/8** (`sexeduc`) ⇒ **the "rift" spoken of here is throughout the rift on this one item, not a general property of those two groups.** But "the rift closed" is a sentence I have not earned: I measured both sides moving the same way, not the distance between them actually shrinking**  `[#839「天花板偏的方向和我担心的相反」]` (Entry 839) 〔GSS〕 ⟨gates: ① threshold control (strictly increasing, fitted shares recovered to **5.6e−17**) ⇒ pass; ② positive control: a +0.30 shift on the latent scale is recovered as **+0.3050** with **+0.0000 leakage into the other stratum** ⇒ pass; ③ negative control: a world moving strictly uniformly on the latent scale ⇒ **+0.0160 / +0.0034** (⚠ **this zero should be zero**, but it passes through one resampling discretisation ⇒ **it carries sampling noise and is not an analytic zero**, stated rather than glossed) ⇒ pass; ④ premise: all 10 cells rerun, not only the threatened one ⇒ pass; ⑤ pre-registered kill ⇒ pass; `Gate.admissible()`=**True**; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: ① **the thresholds τ=[0.291,0.404,0.584] sit very close together** — the two middle categories hold only **4.2% and 6.4%**, so this "latent scale" is driven mostly by the 61%/28% split at the ends and **is close to a binary**; ② fixing the thresholds **assumes the option wordings meant the same thing for fifty years**, and **that is a written assumption, not a tested conclusion** ⇒ what this round adjudicates is "**under the assumption of stable wording**, can the ceiling still explain it away", not "the ceiling cannot explain it"⟩|
| **Give the gap its own interval: "it was pulled open" holds, "it closed again" cannot be said** | **`#770` established this long ago: compare the quantity itself, not two quantities each against zero** — `#838`/`#839` reported the **two strata's own** departures, and I read "first pulled open, then caught up" out of them; **but "the rift widened / the rift closed" is a claim about the GAP, and the gap's interval had never been computed** ⇒ compute `dep_gap`'s own 95% bootstrap interval (B=4000), **both rulers side by side whether or not they agree** | **Mean scale: 1990s −0.5129 [−0.740,−0.287] excludes · 2000s −0.3601 [−0.599,−0.129] excludes · 2010s +0.1384 [−0.093,+0.364] contains zero**; **latent scale: 1990s −0.3296 [−0.601,−0.035] excludes · 2000s −0.3396 [−0.587,−0.101] excludes · 2010s +0.0854 [−0.158,+0.331] contains zero** ⇒ verdict **OVERTURNED** (controls sound, the pre-registered kill fired against expectation), `Gate.admissible()`=**True**; ⚠ **`G3` requires a stricter statement**: over the 10-cell family BH and BY each leave **3**, and **`latent 1990s` is not among them (p=0.0300, short of BH's rank threshold)** ⇒ **"the nineties widening" holds on both rulers under the interval criterion but only on the mean scale under BH — both sentences get written**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, more cautious than last round's and also longer: the rift was pulled open in the nineties and kept widening right through the 2000s, and that holds; but "it closed again afterwards" I cannot say — both sides did move toward greater permissiveness in the 2010s, yet whether the distance between them actually shrank is something this data cannot answer with an interval excluding zero. "Both sides are moving" and "the distance shrank" are not the same sentence, and last round I nearly treated them as one** `[#840「给差距算它自己的区间」]` (Entry 840) 〔GSS〕 ⟨**⚠⚠⚠ three methodological notes, and all three are the controls catching me rather than me catching them**: ① **the identity control first failed at 2.3e−5, and what failed was my retyping of `#838`'s four decimal places** ⇒ changed to **reading `#838`'s own results JSON**, after which the difference is 0.00e+00 ⇒ **rule: never type a previous round's number into the next round's script; read it from the artifact** (registered as `#840`①); ② **one of this round's stated reasons for existing was itself false** — I claimed "the two strata are cut from the same year's sample ⇒ their errors are correlated ⇒ the difference's interval cannot be obtained by subtracting the two", **but the devout and secular terciles are disjoint sets of people and their errors are independent by construction**; the first version's `joint=True/False` branches were literally the same operation, giving an SD ratio of exactly **1.0000×** ⇒ **the control did not fail; it was testing a claim false a priori** ⇒ replaced by a prediction that is **independently derivable and could have failed**, `SD(dep_gap)=√(SD_devout²+SD_secular²)`, measured **0.11423 against a predicted 0.11651 = 0.9804×** ⇒ **disjointness confirmed and the "must resample jointly" premise formally withdrawn** (⚠ **this is not changing the criterion after seeing the result**: what replaced it is arithmetic derivable before the run that could have come out otherwise); ③ the second version's identity control was ruled **DEGENERATE** by the library, and **the library was right** (`#773`) — I was comparing `max|difference|` against a literal 0, **both exactly zero, and a computed 0 equalling a hard-coded 0 proves nothing** ⇒ changed to `#770`'s form: **compare two non-zero values** (−0.512923 vs −0.512923); positive control exact at +0.2500, negative control **+1.47e−15** (no discretisation ⇒ **an analytic zero**); ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: ① **the 2010s cell containing zero is not evidence that the rift did not close, only that this data cannot resolve it** — the interval half-width is ≈0.23 against a quantity of ≈0.09, so **the noise is 2.5× what must be resolved**, and `#835` already established this as the structural limit of decade resolution (GSS runs 4–6 times per decade), **not a compute problem**; | **Building a ruler for "never retype last round's number" — and the first one was killed by its own null** | **`#840`① asked that "never retype a previous round's number" become a tool rather than a resolution (`#836`①'s same sentence: a lesson that does not reach the tooling has not landed)** ⇒ the first proxy was **value matching**: a literal with ≥4 decimals in a script that equals, or is the rounding of, some value in a `results/*.json` ⇒ flagged as transcribed; **the positive control passed (plant a copied value, it fires) — and I nearly shipped it on that basis** | ⚠⚠⚠ **but `realstat` carries a rule aimed exactly here: "a positive control asks whether the instrument can see, never whether what it sees is the thing you are about to claim about"** ⇒ so I measured its **false-positive rate**: 4000 **random** literals against the real artifacts (11,376 finite values, 90.6% of them in |v|<1) ⇒ **4 decimals 32.0% · 5 decimals 4.2% · 6 decimals 0.4% · 8 decimals 0.0%**; **the first version reported 1057 hits and most were coincidence** — visible without any statistics: **literals in `E01/R001` were flagged as copied from `E03/R195` and `R265`, rounds that did not exist until several hundred rounds later**; ⚠ **and raising the threshold to 6 decimals does not save it**: a 6-decimal hit is a **full-precision copy**, which is numerically harmless, whereas what actually went wrong in `#840` was **`0.6266 ← 0.626612`, a four-decimal rounding — sitting precisely in the 32% band** ⇒ **value matching structurally cannot catch the error it was built to catch, and no parameter fixes that**; ⇒ **the second version changes the proxy from the value to the provenance** (a reference to another round's id, a literal with ≥3 significant decimals nearby, and **never a `json.load` of that round's artifact**), and measurement cut it three more times, **890 → 591 → 372 → 357**, all three the same error (**a rule covering only the standard form fails silently on the non-standard form**, and every failure direction was **over-reporting**): ① only `body[0]`'s docstring was skipped, but docstrings are often not at `body[0]` (imports come first) ⇒ skip string-constant statements **anywhere**; ② comments were not skipped, though a comment is a **citation**, not a **consumption** ⇒ skip COMMENT tokens via `tokenize`; ③ **a zero-padded round number is an author's choice, not a machine's output** (`-0.2000` is really `-0.2`) ⇒ skip anything with fewer than 3 significant decimals after stripping trailing zeros; ⇒ **a sentence about method, worth more than the ruler itself: I built a ruler to catch retyping, its positive control fired, and three times in ten it was convicting random numbers. A positive control can only tell you the instrument is not blind; it will never tell you the instrument has the wrong suspect — for that you must feed it things you know to be innocent and count how many it convicts** `[#841「第一把被自己的零杀掉了」]` (Entry 841) 〔this project's own scripts〕 ⟨gates: positive control (a copied artifact value must be caught) ⇒ pass; **negative control α** (a script that cites another round **and does read** its artifact) ⇒ no hit, pass; **negative control β** (only author-chosen round numbers 0.05/0.30/6000) ⇒ no hit, pass; **when the controls fail the tool exits 2 and refuses to certify** (★P5: an instrument never shown to fire gives silence, not an acquittal); wired into `readme_gate --precommit` as a **ratchet, not zero tolerance** — the opposite of `debts_gate`, for a statable reason: a substantial share of the 357 historical hits are **legitimate citations**, and zero tolerance would force edits to hundreds of old files (`L81`); ⚠ **357 is not "357 errors"** — it is **a queue awaiting human judgement and I have judged none of it** ⇒ **it must not be quoted as this project's defect count** (`#800`: survival is not evidence, and neither is a backlog)⟩|

| **Auditing the three walls I wrote: one fell, and it was the one I was surest of** | **The reason is what happened in `#843`**: I registered a "structurally cannot be fixed" in `#842` and **overturned it the very next round with one `git show`** ⇒ `#843`② registered "go back and check every 'structurally impossible' already written", because **a wall closes off an entire line of research and never gets audited**; ⚠ **classify before testing — three kinds of wall need three different tools**: **DERIVATION** (forced by algebra) can only be checked against what the algebra actually says · **MEASUREMENT** (computed) can be remeasured under another design · **BELIEF** (never checked) is settled by one query — **and `#842`'s wall was a BELIEF disguised as a DERIVATION, the most dangerous kind**; ⚠⚠ **the pre-written confound: I have an incentive for the walls to stand** (a fallen wall means going back to do more work) ⇒ **every test was designed so that "it falls" is easier than "it stands"** — **a wall still standing under that bias is stronger evidence, not weaker** | **W1 `#835` APC non-identification (DERIVATION) ⇒ fell**: build a world with only a nonlinear age effect and zero cohort effect, fit age dummies + year dummies, and the recovered age curve matches the truth at **corr = +0.9995 after removing the linear part** (negative control **+0.103**) ⇒ **`cohort = year − age` makes only the LINEAR components collinear; the nonlinear components are identified. `#835`'s "removing age also removes cohort ⇒ APC inseparable" was overstated and is withdrawn here**; ⚠ **but this topples only the first wall** — `#835`'s second (the three-band stratified design **has no power**, which was measured) **was not touched** ⇒ **what reopens is not the original question but a different route (a pooled age-dummy + year-dummy regression), whose power is a separate unmeasured question**; **W2 `#837` (BELIEF) ⇒ stands**: the widest word list (12 stems) across all **1,781** variables hits only `SCCS176`/`SCCS177`, **one source**; **W3 `#840` (MEASUREMENT) ⇒ stands**: 5-year bins give a half-width of **0.2362**, 10-year **0.2303**, 20-year **not applicable**, and none is pushed to exclude zero; ⚠⚠ **and I knocked W3 down on the first attempt, which was my own selection artifact, caught in self-audit**: the first version chose the bucket by "inside the window with the most years", so `bins=20` selected **[2021, 2022, 2024]** — **that is the 2020s, not the 2010s, and the comparison is simply not the same estimand** ⇒ **precisely `realstat`'s most expensive error: an attack that appears to succeed retracts something true**; ⇒ **a sentence about method: I believed all three walls, and after checking, one fell — the one I was surest of, because it was algebra, and algebra makes checking feel unnecessary. | **The algebraic wall fell; the estimation wall stands — the identifiable half of age is itself inside the noise** | **`#844`① pre-registered this: the wall fell, so is the route passable?** — and `#844` itself wrote "'the wall fell' must not be read as 'the question can be answered'", since **`#835`'s second wall (power) had never been touched** ⇒ **the first thing this round does is not seek a result but measure power** (`#835`'s lesson: the power gate goes ahead of every judgement about people); ⚠⚠ **a wall that fell only halfway forces an estimand that goes only halfway**: one cannot say "remove the age effect", because **age's linear component is collinear with period and cohort, and removing it means removing something unidentifiable** ⇒ **remove only the nonlinear part of the age profile**, **so what this round can ask is smaller than what `#834`① asked — and that is not conservatism, it is the only thing identified** | **⓪ the power gate passed**: planting +0.2552 into the devout stratum's last 1990s year moves `dep_gap` from −0.5105 to −0.2552, **recovered at ratio 1.000, worth 2.17 bootstrap SDs** ⇒ the design has power; **⚠⚠⚠ and the negative control stopped it before any result was read, very cleanly** — comparing the nonlinear range of the real profile against a **sham** profile estimated after **permuting the age labels**: **1-year bands (72 dummies) real 0.3744 / sham 0.3305 = 0.883 · 5-year bands (15 dummies) real 0.1479 / sham 0.1675 = 1.132 · 10-year bands (8 dummies) real 0.1106 / sham 0.1037 = 0.937** ⇒ **not one of the three resolutions is usable**; ⇒ **this is a sharper version of `#835`'s second wall, and it says more than the original "no power": it is not that the quantity is measured imprecisely, it is that the identifiable part does not rise above its own noise — so there is nothing to adjust for. | **"The non-religious moved faster" is not a sentence about those people — it holds only on this one item** | **`#838`'s own NEXT pre-registered this round**: "do not extrapolate 'the seculars left' to other items — this round only ran `homosex`" ⇒ **this is a scope test of a claim about people**, and scope is exactly what `§2` names as the cause of death for **eleven of twelve retractions: a correct number reported without the range over which it holds**; **the two scopes are two entirely different psychologies**: **S1, about people** — the secular stratum moved faster than its own pace **generally** in the nineties, and homosexuality is merely one item they passed through; **S2, about the item** — the secular stratum moved only on this item, in which case what happened in the nineties **belongs to the question, not the group** | **Eight items × two strata, the 1990s departure (B=4000, each cell's own 95% bootstrap interval; gates: ② **positive control**: planting +0.30 into `homosex`'s devout stratum moves that cell **+0.3000** and **the other stratum +0.0000** ⇒ pass; ③ **negative control**: a world where both strata move strictly uniformly ⇒ **−7.4e−16 / +2.8e−17** (⚠ **this zero should be zero**, and with no discretisation it is an **analytic zero**) ⇒ pass; ④ **premise (the pre-written confound)**: the eight items differ widely in headroom and `#838` had measured only `homosex` ⇒ headroom printed per cell with anything above 60% flagged `CEILING?` and excluded from the verdict — **0/16 fired**, so **this 1/8 is not manufactured by a ceiling**; ⑤ **the kill fails** ⇒ verdict **OVERTURNED**, `Gate.admissible()`=**True**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, and it narrows last round's: "the non-religious moved faster" is not a sentence about those people. A repaired tool is not the same as a tool I use; for it to bite, it has to appear at the **verification** step, not only the **editing** step** `[#847「又一次落在两条路的一条上」]` (Entry 847) 〔this project's own pages〕 ⟨gate: the structural recount — **4 units per page, 4 carrying the note, 0 missing** (printed per page); ⚠ **this round has no positive or negative control, stated rather than faked**: it is not a measurement — **the criterion is "every unit containing that anchor carries the note", a predicate verifiable by exhaustion, not an estimand needing a null distribution**; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: the notes guarantee only that **the page says the scope right now**, **not that the next entry I write will say it** — **the other face of what `#843` measured: a correction that has "landed" is a state that decays**, and nothing prevents me writing another over-scoped sentence tomorrow, **registered rather than pretended solved** (⇒ `#847`①: add the mechanical check to `readme_gate`)⟩|
| **A scope gate — and the class it cannot catch is written into the gate's own docstring** | **Paying `#847`①** — turning "when citing a `homosex`-only conclusion you must state the scope" **from something I remember into a mechanical check** (`#836`①/`#840`①'s same sentence: **a lesson that does not reach the tooling has not landed**); `tools/scope_gate.py` defines its population **structurally** via `lib/page_units.units()` (the one `#842` repaired — and `#847` went wrong three times precisely by not using it), with the criterion "a unit cites `#838`/`#839`/`#840`/`#846` and contains no scope word", **wired into `readme_gate --precommit` as a ratchet rather than zero tolerance** | ⚠⚠ **while building the gate, the instrument stopped me twice, and both are worth recording**: **① the positive control's fixture disarmed the positive control** — the first "no scope" sample was written `⟨无作用域⟩`, **and that string itself contains the scope word 作用域**, so it was judged "scope stated" and **the control did not fire** ⇒ the same shape as `feedback_check_encodes_instance_not_property`: **the fixture decided the verdict**; rewritten as `⟨没有说范围⟩`, it fires; **② one identifier with two referents, and the notation I expected to separate them does not** — the first scan returned **8 hits**, all of one kind: those units cite **`#840`①** (the debt "never retype last round's number"), **not `#840`'s conclusion about the rift**, so I added the distinction "debts are written `#NNN①`, conclusions bare `#NNN`" together with **negative control γ (a unit citing only `#840①` must not fire — it passes)**, **and the total is still 8** — those four entries (×2 pages) also cite the **transcription incident** with a bare `#840` ⇒ **this instrument cannot separate the two referents, so the baseline is set at 8 and those 8 are named explicitly as known false positives**; ⇒ **a sentence about method: an identifier in this project can denote two things — a conclusion about people and an incident about me — and the page's notation writes both as the same string. I tried to separate them by notation and failed; so the failure goes into the gate's docstring rather than the baseline being set to 0 to pretend it away. `#841` taught this: a tool that over-reports gets ignored, and an ignored tool is no tool — so false positives get named, not tolerated and not hidden** `[#848「写在闸的文件头上」]` (Entry 848) 〔this project's own pages and its own tools〕 ⟨**Production**; the gate's own gates: positive control (a citation without scope must be caught) **pass** · negative α (cites and states scope) **pass** · negative β (cites nothing) **pass** · **negative γ (cites only the debt id `#840①`) pass**; **when the controls fail the tool exits 2 and refuses to certify** (★P5: an instrument never shown to fire gives silence, not an acquittal); ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: **those 8 are not "8 errors"** but **a backlog of known false positives**; the ratchet blocks only **new** ones, and clearing a new one costs a single `homosex` or "scope" in the sentence ⇒ **8 must not be quoted as this project's defect count** (`#800`/`#841`: survival is not evidence, and neither is a backlog); ⚠ **the real fix is to type the identifiers, not to patch the regex** — if the page wrote "conclusion" and "incident" as distinguishable citations this false positive would vanish by itself, **but that is a whole-page rewrite whose cost and benefit are unmeasured** ⇒ **registered as an idea, not a plan** (`#848`①)⟩|

**the numbers are recorded but, per the pre-registration, not interpreted** (reading them with the gate failed is simply bypassing the gate): full sample +0.0720 [+0.015,+0.127] (own permutation floor 0.0690, above) · sex=male n=4,209 **+0.0655** (floor 0.1038, below) · sex=female n=7,308 **+0.0771** (floor 0.0813, below) · even nineties years n=10,892 **+0.0720** (floor 0.0676, **above**) · **odd years unusable** (too few qualifying years within the decade) ⇒ usable 3/4 · same sign 3/3 · above own floor **1/3**; ⚠ **"all three halves agree in sign" looks like support and is not** — **agreement in sign is near-certain in a design without power, because the three halves share most of the same people**; ⇒ **a sentence about method: I wanted to rescue a multiplicity-rejected cell by looking at half the people, and that route could have been shown unworkable before touching it — halve the sample and the smallest detectable effect grows larger than the effect being tested.
