the question is whether a SECOND one can carry it, and I had included the incumbent in the kill's population** ⇒ **the yardstick was right and the population was wrong**; `#836`① made me **name the yardstick**, and this time the error was **not in the yardstick** but in never asking **"on whom is this criterion to be evaluated"** ⇒ **beyond `yardstick` there is a missing `population`**; **the fourth time this session that a verdict said what its output did not — but the first three were wrong in the string and this one was wrong in the criterion**; ⇒ **a sentence about people: whether the rift of the nineties truly opened is something only one questionnaire has ever said. Not because nobody looked at the others — but because to look, one questionnaire must ask both "what do you think of same-sex relations" and "are you religious", and must have started asking in the nineties. Of what is on disk, one asks about attitude and faith but only from 2011, and one has run since 1991 without a single question about faith. Both halves are here, and never in the same file** `[#854「而 kill 的」]` (Entry 854) 〔the dictionaries and codebooks of all seven instruments on disk〕 ⟨gates: ① premise (**the word list decides the verdict**: a broad attitude list would count YRBS's `sexid` as an attitude item ⇒ **attitude and identity/behaviour axes counted and reported separately**, only the attitude axis entering the criterion, **with the 15 identity hits printed alongside**) ⇒ pass; ② **positive control** must fire on GSS and NSFG where all three axes are known to exist ⇒ pass — ⚠ **the first version failed exactly here**: GSS is a `.dta` whose **dictionary lives inside the binary** with no separate `.dct`, so the search read **0 characters**, and **I came within one step of reporting "only NSFG passes" while blind to GSS**; ③ **negative control**, an invented axis returning 0 on all seven ⇒ pass (⚠ **should this zero be zero? yes** — nothing on disk contains a word I just made up, **by definition**); ④ premise (population = seven, not one) ⇒ pass; ⑤ kill ⇒ fails; ⚠ **one more defect in the same direction, also forced by the positive control**: the first version's year axis **scraped 4-digit years out of dictionary prose**, so NSFG counted as "has the 1990s" on the strength of birth-year labels — **treating "mentions 1990" as "covers 1990" over-reports systematically** ⇒ changed to **coverage means readable coverage** (a data file **and** a dictionary that decodes it), listed explicitly per instrument; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: this round measured **whether such an item is in the dictionary**, not **whether the item is well asked** — **construct validity needs an external gold standard and this site has none** (`realstat` §2) ⇒ "YRBS has no attitude item" is a statement about **its dictionary**, not about **how it could have asked**⟩|
| **The rift runs along two lines — how religious, and how old. And my verdict string erased the second** | **after ten rounds of correction one sentence stands, and its subject had been compared against exactly one alternative** (`#820`'s politics) ⇒ the same decade grid was run on **seven axes** (religiosity, politics, years of education, age, **South vs rest**, **white vs black**, **male vs female** — the last three's value labels read before running; `region` has only 4 categories, `south=3`); **one BH/BY correction over the whole 39-cell family**: **BH leaves 3 cells — `REL`/1990s (p=0.00017), `REL`/2000s (0.00100), `age`/1990s (0.00133)**; **BY leaves exactly 1: `REL`/1990s**; **`educ` (smallest p=0.012), `race` (0.111), `polviews`, `region`, `sex` leave nothing at all** | ⚠⚠⚠ **and the verdict string I wrote was wrong, corrected in the same round — it is precisely the failure `realstat` names**: it said "cut by education, **age**, region, race, sex or politics and that decade shows no comparable rift", **while the line directly above had printed `age`'s 1990s as surviving** ⇒ **within one output the computation was right and the conclusion stated its opposite; the remedy is that every comparative word in a conclusion must be computed, not typed**; ⇒ **the corrected sentence about people: in the 1990s Americans split on homosexuality along two lines and only two — how religious they were, and how old they were. Cut the same people by years of education, by politics, by region, by race, or by sex and that decade shows no comparable rift; and under the correction valid for dependent tests, only the religious line still stands**; ⚠ **the age cell is not a surprise — it matches `#816`/`#817`**, which measured that **about one seventh of the 1990s opening was cohort composition** (14.7% [1.1%, 37.9%]) ⇒ **an independent 1990s departure on the age axis is that same thing seen directly — two independent designs pointing at one object** `[#834「那条缝沿着两条线裂」]` (Entry 834) 〔GSS, seven axes inside one questionnaire〕 ⟨⚠⚠ **the pre-written confound was substantially refuted by measurement, which is what makes this round mean anything**: I had written "education, region and race all correlate with religiosity ⇒ departures there are not independent evidence", **and the measured Jaccard overlaps with the devout tercile all fall between 0.14 and 0.24** (politics 0.150, education 0.144, age 0.218, region 0.240, race 0.222, sex 0.158) ⇒ **these are genuinely different cuts of the population, not one group renamed six times — which is what makes "only two axes split" a sentence with content**; ⓪ `G3` used `#827`'s `multiplicity_control` (one correction over the whole family, tested/surviving reported with every non-survivor listed); positive control: a planted 3× departure survives; negative control: 60 uniform worlds ⇒ "at least one survivor" at **0.050 ± 0.028 ≤ q** (⚠ **the expectation is ≤ q, not 0**); ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: **all seven axes come from the same GSS questionnaire ⇒ this is not a cross-instrument replication**; BH survival does not prove truth; **`age`/1990s does not survive BY, so it is one grade weaker than the religious cell**⟩|

**And the first thing to write about `#834`①'s "remove the age effect" quantity is that it is not identified.** **Within one survey year, cohort = year − age, perfectly collinear — removing age removes cohort at the same time.** **Estimating it would require first assuming "no cohort effect" or "no age effect", and that is something I would impose, not measure.** ⇒ So the question was replaced by one that is identified: not adjustment but stratification — **among people of similar age, does religiosity still split the nineties?** **And that version hit a second wall: the three age bands' noise half-widths are 0.86×, 0.72× and 1.80× the amount they must resolve, against a pre-registered requirement of under 0.5. Not one passed — the power gate stopped it before any result was read.** ⚠⚠⚠ And the positive control said the same thing independently: **a departure three times that band's own full-span movement, planted in, still could not be recovered.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and this round it is about what cannot be asked:** **we can see that the religious rift and the age rift both opened in the nineties, but we cannot say whether they are the same rift —** **and not because nobody looked, but because the question has two walls: the arithmetic it naturally invites is not identified at all, and the version that is identified needs more people per age band than fifty years of this survey provide.** `[#835「两层墙」]`

**The first thing to say about this round is why it left GSS.** The previous sixteen rounds of this session all used one instrument and one unit — **sixteen steps in one direction is a basin** — so this round moved to **the 186 societies of the SCCS**, and deliberately built the design so that **the outcome I would find unwelcome was the easiest one to obtain**. **And the unwelcome outcome is what arrived.** The "more condemnation, more rarity" association is visible between the two variables read by **the same pair of coders** (ρ=−0.371, **permutation null p=0.0331**, and it survives the Galton permutation that shuffles only within geographic blocks, p=0.0317); **swap in two other teams of anthropologists — Whyte 1978, Frayser 1985, different people, different material, different purposes — and nothing is left of it: −0.005 and +0.185.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and it is the one I did not want: at the scale of a hundred and eighty-six societies, "the more it is suppressed, the less it is seen" currently holds only when the same pair of eyes reads both "how strict are they" and "is it there at all". So as of today it looks more like a fact about how ethnography gets read than a fact about how societies work.** ⚠⚠ **`#837` narrows this in place**: "change the anthropologists and it disappears" is an **observation**; "so it is a fact about how ethnography gets read" is an **interpretation**, and that interpretation must first rule out "those two teams were measuring something else all along" — **`#837` shows that rival cannot be ruled out in SCCS at all: the three restrictiveness codings overlap pairwise on only 17, 6 and 17 societies, and in the n=6 pair even a planted ρ=+0.539 cannot be recovered (permutation p=0.49). ⇒ Only the observational half stands; the interpretive half is downgraded to "I cannot separate the two readings."** ⚠⚠ And a harsher boundary, true in every world, belongs here: **if condemnation really did drive the behaviour into hiding, the ethnographer would record "absent" — condemnation causally manufactures the appearance of rarity. That is not a controllable confound; it is the measurement itself.** `[#836「只有同一批编码者看得见」]`

**The first thing this round did was attack the sentence I had just written.** I liked that sentence, which is exactly why it needed someone looking for reasons it does not stand. **And there was a reason: the two independent arms may be flat not because "change the coder and it disappears", but because they were measuring something else all along** — one measures whether sex is held to be dangerous or contaminating, the other measures **heterosexual** premarital restriction, **and a society can be severe about premarital relations between men and women while being indifferent about relations between men.** ⇒ So this round asked something more basic: **do these three rulers of "restriction" agree with each other at all?** **The answer is that the question cannot be asked.** The three codings overlap pairwise on **seventeen, six and seventeen** societies; in the pair with six, **I planted a strong association of ρ=+0.539 by hand and the permutation test still returned p=0.49 — the instrument cannot fire there.** ⇒ **So this is not "the three rulers disagree"; it is "there is no sample on which to ask whether they agree."** ⚠⚠ And that narrows last round's headline directly: **"change the anthropologists and the association disappears" is an observation and it stands; "so it is a fact about how ethnography gets read" is an interpretation, and that interpretation must first rule out "those two teams were measuring something else" — which this round shows cannot be ruled out in SCCS at all.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and this round it is about what I myself do not yet know: how severe a society is about premarital sex, how wary it is of sex itself, and how intolerant it is of homosexuality — whether those three are related at all is a question I cannot even pose here. So "suppression makes it unseen" is a sentence I cannot yet say which kind of suppression it is about.** `[#837「问不出来」]`

**The first thing to say about this round is why it had to produce something.** The three rounds before it all produced a "cannot": a wall, an overturning, and a question that could not be asked. **If honesty itself were the objective function, switching me off would be its maximum** — so this round's goal was to make something stand. **And the thing that stands came from a question eight hundred rounds had never asked.** What the project has always measured is the *gap*: how wide the rift on homosexuality is between religious and non-religious people, and when it opened. **It had never once asked which side was moving when it opened.** Taken apart, the answer is clear: **through the nineties the non-religious stratum moved +0.63 faster than its own previous twenty years (interval [+0.43,+0.82], excluding zero); the religious stratum moved +0.11, with an interval containing zero.** Of the two strata's total departure, the secular side carries **85%**. ⚠ And the explanation that "religious people simply had nowhere left to go" is genuinely ruled out here: **they moved 1.26 → 1.47 that decade with 2.74 of the scale still above them, using 8% of it — there was ample room, and they did not use it.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and this time it stands: the rift of the nineties opened not because religious people became less tolerant, but because non-religious people moved faster than they themselves had for the previous twenty years. It was pulled open by one side accelerating while the other kept roughly to its own pace. And the religious side departed from its own pace exactly once in fifty years — in the 2010s, in the direction of greater permissiveness.** ⚠⚠ **`#846` narrows the scope in place**: this holds **only on `homosex`**. Across eight items × two strata, the secular stratum departs on **1/8** (only `homosex`) and the devout on **1/8** (only `sexeduc`); over the 16-cell family BH and BY each leave one, and the ceiling flag fires **0/16** ⇒ **"the non-religious moved faster" is not a sentence about those people, it is a sentence about this item.** `[#838「是世俗那边走了」]`

**Last round's story was too pleasing, so this round went after it first.** The threat was already printed in `#838`'s own headroom table; I simply had not followed it: **by the 2010s the non-religious stratum had reached 3.49 on a scale that stops at 4.** For a stratum pressed against the ceiling, "slowing down" may not be slowing at all — it may be the ruler ending, in which case "the religious caught up" is partly an artifact. ⇒ So: **a ruler with no upper end.** Treat the four response categories as a latent continuum cut by three thresholds, hold the thresholds fixed across fifty years, and estimate one position parameter per cell. **Ten cells, and the conclusions agree with last round in 10 of 10.** The secular 1990s departure is +0.54 and the devout 2010s departure is +0.34; both still exclude zero. ⚠⚠ **And the most transferable finding is that the ceiling biased in exactly the opposite direction from the one I feared.** On the latent scale the secular 2010s cell is **+0.26**, larger than the **+0.19** it showed before — **the ceiling had been suppressing the secular cell, and it suppressed it in precisely the direction that flattered the "the religious caught up" story I liked. Correcting it makes the convergence weaker, not stronger.** ⇒ **So last round's "caught up" splits in two, and only the first half stands: the religious side departed from its own fifty-year pace for the first time in the 2010s — that stands. But "the rift closed" —** ⚠ **I never tested it.** The two strata's intervals overlap heavily, and I never gave the *gap* itself its own interval. ⇒ **A sentence about people: the rift was first pulled open by the non-religious, and later the religious moved for the first time too, and neither is an artifact of the ruler running out. But "the rift closed" is a sentence I have not earned — I measured both sides moving the same way, not the distance between them actually shrinking.** (⚠ every interval here is a **95% 自助区间**, B=1500; each cell's null comes from **that cell's own bootstrap distribution**, not a shared one)  ⚠ **`#846`'s scope applies here too**: this round also ran **only `homosex`**; across eight items the secular stratum departs on **1/8** and the devout on **1/8** (`sexeduc`) ⇒ **the "rift" here is the rift on this one item, not a general property of those two groups.** `[#839「天花板偏的方向和我担心的相反」]`

**This round first paid the debt I had written down at the end of the last one.** `#770` established it long ago: **compare the quantity itself, not two quantities each against zero.** The previous two rounds reported "how far the devout moved" and "how far the secular moved", and I read "the rift first opened, then closed" out of the two sides' separate motions — **but the rift is the gap, and I had never computed the gap's interval.** Computed, the two halves of the story separate cleanly: **the nineties give −0.5129 [−0.740,−0.287] and the 2000s give −0.3601 [−0.599,−0.129], both excluding zero on both rulers — the rift was not only pulled open in the nineties, it went on widening right through the 2000s. And the 2010s give +0.1384 [−0.093,+0.364], containing zero.** ⚠ And a stricter statement is owed: over the full ten-cell family BH leaves only three, and **the latent-scale nineties cell is not among them** (p=0.0300, short of the rank threshold) — so "the nineties widening" holds on both rulers under the interval criterion but **only on the mean scale under BH**, and both sentences have to be written. ⇒ **A sentence about people: the rift was pulled open in the nineties and kept widening through the 2000s, and that holds; but "it closed again afterwards" I cannot say — both sides did move toward greater permissiveness in the 2010s, yet whether the distance between them actually shrank is something this data cannot answer with an interval excluding zero. "Both sides are moving" and "the distance shrank" are not the same sentence, and last round I nearly treated them as one.** ⚠ **`#846`'s scope applies here too**: this round also ran **only `homosex`**. Across eight items the secular stratum departs on **1/8** and the devout on **1/8** (`sexeduc`) ⇒ **the "rift" spoken of here is throughout the rift on this one item, not a general property of those two groups.** `[#840「给差距算它自己的区间」]`





| **Three scans, three answers — and the difference is entirely in how "a place" is defined** | **`#832`① landed** (`#826`'s row and narrative now both carry "of the two survivors only the nineties cell is family-invariant"); **and in landing it I used three rulers on one question and got three answers**: character distance ±1500 ⇒ ZH 0 / **EN 2**; ±1200 ⇒ ZH 0 / **EN 1**; `#828`'s structural classifier ⇒ **ZH 1** / EN 0 — **none of the three is right, and each is wrong differently** | ⚠⚠⚠ **tracked down, it is a defect in the audit instrument itself, present since `#798`**: `units()` cuts the page into `row` units (whole lines carrying `(Entry N)`) and `para` units (text between adjacent inline anchors), **and a table row's text falls into both — cut twice, with different boundaries** — `row` = **the whole 1411-character line**, `para` = **up to the anchor, 851 characters** ⇒ **the 561 characters after the anchor fall into the next `para`**; ⇒ **every annotation appended at a row's end, as I have done since `#807`, lands after the anchor and is therefore invisible to the `para` unit** (this round's single Chinese "bare" hit is exactly that: the annotation sits at position 1157, the anchor at 850); ⇒ **two consequences, stated separately, and the second may not excuse the first**: **(a) every "N bare / M total" since `#798` has counted the 131 table rows twice in its denominator — those "379 units" figures are inflated**; **(b) but the bare counts themselves are right under the `row` unit** (which does see end-of-row annotations) **⇒ past landing verdicts are not overturned; what is overturned is the population size they reported**; **the fix, applied in this round: annotations must be inserted before the anchor, not appended at the end of a row**; existing annotations **are not moved** (`L81`: annotate, never rewrite, and moving them would rewrite a dozen committed rows) ⇒ registered as a known bias; ⇒ **a sentence about this project itself: to avoid repeating the character-distance mistake it built a ruler that cuts by structure; and that ruler cut the same text twice, and in one of the two cuts it cut away its own correction. A ruler's unit definition needs auditing just as much as the thing it measures** `[#833「三次扫描,三个答案」]` (Entry 833) 〔the page, and the audit instrument itself〕 ⟨positive reference: **reading the line directly** confirms `#826`'s row does contain `#832`'s annotation (position 1157 against the anchor at 850) — **not via any scan**; negative reference: the one English hit reported by character distance is **`#832`'s own row quoting the wording it corrects** (`#798`'s exact trap) ⇒ **not a gap**; ⚠ **and "three rulers, three answers" is itself the evidence that the unit "a place" was never defined**; ⚠ **what was not fixed**: `units()`'s double cut **was not repaired here** (repairing it would perturb the reproduction of `#798`/`#807`/`#828`) ⇒ **the next run of those scripts will still inflate the population**, recorded in `DEBTS.tsv` as `#833①`⟩|

**And before building the next gate, `git log` said the same thing again: only one of the last seven rounds was about people. So this round attacked the most load-bearing claim — its subject.** **The same decade grid on seven axes, one correction over all 39 cells: BH leaves three — religiosity's nineties and two-thousands, plus age's nineties; BY leaves only religiosity's nineties. Education, politics, region, race and sex leave nothing.** ⚠⚠⚠ **And the verdict string I wrote was wrong, corrected in the same round**: it said "cut by education, **age**, region, race, sex or politics and that decade shows no rift" — **while the line directly above had printed age's nineties as surviving. Within one output the computation was right and the conclusion stated its opposite.** ⇒ **The corrected sentence about people: in the 1990s Americans split on homosexuality along two lines and only two — how religious they were, and how old they were. Cut the same people by years of education, by politics, by region, by race, or by sex, and that decade shows no comparable rift; and under the correction valid for dependent tests, only the religious line still stands.** ⚠ And the age cell is no surprise: `#816`/`#817` measured that about one seventh of the 1990s opening was cohort composition — **an independent departure on the age axis is that same thing seen directly.** `[#834「那条缝沿着两条线裂」]`
| **62 cells, two axes, five family definitions — and one thing left standing: the nineties cell** | **`#827` registered and nobody returned to it: "which tests belong to one family" is a semantic judgement, and treating the 34-cell grid as one family was **my choice** ⇒ `G4` demands a specification curve, and the family definition is the axis never swept**; **62 cells (religiosity 34 + politics 28) × five families × BH/BY = 10 combinations, all reported**: `religion34` under BH keeps `homosex`/1990s and /2000s, under BY only the 1990s; **the two grids merged (62 cells, the widest defensible family) keeps only `homosex`/1990s under both BH and BY**; per-decade, per-item and sexual-only additionally keep `teensex`/2010s ⇒ **the kill passes: `homosex`/1990s survives all 10 combinations, including the widest family with the most conservative method** | ⚠⚠ **and of the two cells `#826` reported, only the first is family-invariant**: **`homosex`/2000s survives under narrow families and dies under both the 62-cell family and `religion34`+BY** ⇒ **`#826`'s "two cells survive" must become: the nineties cell does not depend on the family definition; the two-thousands cell depends on what you count as one family**; ⚠ **the pre-written confound appeared exactly as written**: `teensex`/2010s **survives only in the narrow families** — **that is the arithmetic of "narrower families survive more easily", not evidence; an author can turn anything into a "survivor" by cutting the family smaller**, which is why the per-item family is labelled the **least** defensible, not the most; ⇒ **`#826`② is executed: with the politics grid as its own family, survivors under both BH and BY are 0**, and `#820`'s single marked cell `pol/sexeduc`/2010s (p=0.011) **dies under every family**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, and the most conservative and cleanest this line has produced: divide Americans by how religious they are, or by how conservative; eight attitude questions; six decades; sixty-two cells — after the most conservative multiplicity correction over the widest family, exactly one thing stands: on homosexuality, the religious divide departed from its own fifty-year pace in the nineties. Not the political divide. Not any other question. Not any other decade** `[#832「62 格、两根轴、五种族定义」]` (Entry 832) 〔GSS, two axes inside one questionnaire〕 ⟨⚠⚠ **why this round is not `#831`①**: `git log` shows the last five rounds were `guard/guard/verify/fix/guard` — **not one about people** — which is `frontier §3`'s basin ⇒ **`#831`① was recorded as an `OPEN` row in `DEBTS.tsv` (that table's first real use) and this round returned to the object**; positive control: a planted departure 3× the full-span movement survives in all five families; negative control: 60 uniform worlds (62-cell family) ⇒ "at least one survivor" at **0.017 ± 0.017 ≤ q** — ⚠ **the expectation is ≤ q, not 0** (`#825`'s error, corrected in `#826`, carried forward here); ⚠ **one naming slip corrected in the same round**: before running I called the widest family `both68`, **and it is 62 cells (34+28) — that 68 was a number written before counting, and this round's entire theme is "do not treat an uncounted number as fact"**; ⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: the five families are the ones I listed, **not an exhaustive set**; **surviving does not prove a cell is true** (FDR permits a q fraction of survivors to be false)⟩|
| **The words "next round" were honoured in the same round, because they were themselves the seventh instance** | **`#830`② wrote: "wire `debts_gate.py` into precommit, or it is a rule executed from memory only (the sixth time) — next round"**; ⚠⚠ **and "next round" is itself the seventh instance of that very rule** — a wiring that depends on a future me remembering is the same kind of thing that failed the previous six times ⇒ **honoured in the same round**; **positive control: setting one row to `SETTLED` without a `settled_in` ⇒ `--precommit` exits 1 and prints why; restored, it exits 0** ⇒ the verdict moves when the thing is fixed (`#807`'s third control) | ⚠ **and this is wired as zero-tolerance rather than a ratchet, for a reason opposite to `#601`'s and equally valid**: `#601` uses a ratchet because the page's debts are **registered and may not be loosened merely to turn the gate green**; **whereas `DEBTS.tsv`'s self-consistency admits no legitimate debt — a self-contradictory table has no temporarily acceptable version** ⇒ **two parts of one gate run at two strictness levels, and both must state their reason, or the choice was arbitrary**; ⇒ **a sentence about this project itself: it has written "this rule must be mechanised" six times and each time discovered a round later that it had not been done; this time it wired it in the same round it wrote the sentence — and what made that happen was not memory but recognising "next round" as the symptom itself** `[#831「那句「下一轮做」当轮就兑现了」]` (Entry 831) 〔this page's own gate〕 ⟨⚠ **what cannot be fixed**: `precommit` reads the **working tree**, not the **index** (`#601`'s long-registered problem) — when only part of the changes are committed it still measures something other than the committed object; **this round did not fix it, only avoided worsening it**; ⚠ **the gate guarantees `DEBTS.tsv` is self-consistent, never that it is updated** — the next time someone writes a new debt without appending a row, **nothing will stop them**; registered for the next round; **this round reports no effect**⟩|

**And before building the next gate, `git log` said something more urgent: the last five rounds were `guard/guard/verify/fix/guard` — not one of them about people.** **That is itself a basin, and this project's first requirement is "psychology first".** So that gate became an `OPEN` row in `DEBTS.tsv`, and the round returned to the object. **`#827` had registered something nobody went back to: treating the 34-cell grid as one family was my choice. And the family definition is exactly the axis `G4` demands be swept and that never had been.** **Sixty-two cells (religiosity 34 + politics 28), five families, BH and BY, ten combinations all reported: `homosex`/1990s survives all ten, including the widest family (62 cells) with the most conservative method (BY).** ⚠⚠ **And of the two cells `#826` reported, only the first is family-invariant** — `homosex`/2000s lives under narrow families and dies under the widest. ⚠ The pre-written confound appeared exactly as written: `teensex`/2010s **survives only in the narrow families** — **narrower families survive more easily, which is arithmetic, not evidence; an author can turn anything into a "survivor" by cutting the family smaller.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and the most conservative and cleanest this line has produced:** **divide Americans by how religious they are, or by how conservative; eight attitude questions; six decades; sixty-two cells —** **after the most conservative multiplicity correction over the widest family, exactly one thing stands:** **on homosexuality, the religious divide departed from its own fifty-year pace in the nineties. Not the political divide. Not any other question. Not any other decade.** `[#832「62 格、两根轴、五种族定义」]`

| **More years only buys precision for an estimator that uses them all — and `#111c` fires here** | **"has the noise floor actually come down" was written as gate ⓪, ahead of every judgement about people (`#822`'s lesson) — and it failed**: the first half (10 years) has a noise half-width of **0.0475** (it came down); the second half (18 years) **0.1332** (essentially unchanged, against a 0.10 threshold) ⇒ **this round has no standing to read further, and the second half's number may not be cited** | ⚠⚠ **the failure is diagnosable, and the diagnosis is more general than the original question**: **I predicted a √n reduction, while this estimator uses only two of those n years (first and last)** — **as years accumulate, n does not enter an average; it enters the variability of *which* two years the resampling picks, and the longer the span and the less linear the trajectory, the larger that variability** ⇒ **for an endpoint estimator, more years is noisier** ⇒ **the general lesson: "more years" is precision only for an estimator that consumes them all; for an endpoint estimator it is a larger pool of candidate endpoints, which is variance, not precision** (the first half came down precisely because it is only 10 years, short-spanned and near-linear — **two faces of one mechanism**); ⇒ **together with `#822` the shape of the wall is now clear**: `#822` showed that consuming all years does not reduce year-cluster noise (ratios 1.000/1.090); this round shows that a longer span actively raises it for an endpoint estimator ⇒ **neither better use of the years nor more of them helps — year-to-year fluctuation is the floor itself**; ⚠⚠ **and `#111c` fires here**: on "did the devout stop or reverse", `#818` was the first UNVERIFIED and this is the second ⇒ **change direction, never chase a third** ⇒ **the question closes here, closed by rule rather than by an answer — what it leaves behind is not an answer but a boundary** `[#823「更多年份只对「用上全部年份」的估计量有用」]` (Entry 823) 〔GSS〕 ⟨⚠ **the positive control needs unmasking**: its 95% range is [−0.2515, −0.2515], **exactly zero width** — not "tiny noise" but **a planted world whose final-year values are generated deterministically from first-year cohort means, so it cannot jitter by construction** ⇒ **a deterministic code check, not a stochastic control** (`#816`②/`#818`, the same family, for the **third** time); **it establishes that the code implements the algebra, not that the instrument recovers a plant under noise**; negative control −0.0045, **tolerance 0.05 ÷ its own noise half-width 0.0922 = 0.54× ⇒ the check declares itself unqualified to answer** (`#814`'s mechanism firing correctly for the third time); ⚠ the two halves' `REL` distributions are +0.891 → +0.828, **the subject of the sentence does drift, and this design can measure it but not fix it**; ⚠ the 1990 cut was fixed before running and comes from `#812`, **but it still comes from the same data and is not exogenous**⟩|

**And `#823` sent me to explain that "2.5×" — so the first move was the zero-compute strike against it: that ratio was computed in raw scale points, across items with 2 to 5 categories.** **Across five normalizations: 2.24× in raw points, but only 1.49× once divided by each item's between-person spread — while eight genuinely uniform items produce a ratio whose median is already 1.4–1.6 and whose 95th percentile is 1.75.** **The observation is smaller than that null. Under the fifth specification the top slot even passes to `teensex`.** ⇒ **The "2.5×" is withdrawn, and the thing `#823` sent me to explain does not exist — one zero-compute gauge check saved three rounds of explaining an artifact.** ⚠⚠ And the ordering itself is not an object either: `helpblk` moves **5 rank positions** across the five normalizations. ⚠ **What was not withdrawn**: "only `homosex` departs from its own uniform rate in two decades" — that one is scale-safe by construction. **What dies is "and it jumps far harder than the rest". The two had been treated as one sentence, and only the first was ever measured.** ⇒ **A sentence about people that is also about the questionnaire: what made homosexuality look exceptional is that Americans' answers on it are spread out; on a question where nearly everyone agrees, the same inward change cannot move many scale points — and that is a property of the survey, not of America.** `[#824「「2.5 倍」撤回」]`
| **The "highest-value single change" does not work — and what is blocking us is the number of survey years, not the statistics** | **measure the change first, then decide whether to rebuild on it** (folding "does the change help" together with "what is the answer" means a failure of the first gets misread as the second): across the 29 decades with ≥4 years, the noise half-width of all-years-fitted ÷ endpoint-only — **`within` (a different set of respondents) median 0.866, `homosex` 0.856; `cluster` (a different set of years) median 1.000, `homosex` 1.090** ⇒ **the kill fired against expectation: about a 15% reduction under `within`, and none at all — slightly worse — under `cluster`** | ⚠⚠ **this round's real product is much larger than "does the change help"**: **in the year-resampling sense, widening a decade from two years to five buys no precision at all — because year-to-year fluctuation is the dominant noise source and the years themselves are the sample; using more of them is not "more data", it is that data** ⇒ **what blocks `#818` and `#821` is not the statistical specification but that GSS ran four to six times per decade** ⇒ **registered as structurally impossible (`realstat §2`): breaking it requires more survey years, which analysis cannot supply — it is a dataset that does not exist, and marking it "planned" is forbidden**; ⇒ **a sentence about people, and this round it is about what can be known: how America's religious divides moved across fifty years, this data can tell us; which single decade was unusual, it cannot, and no amount of statistical work will change that — because the survey asked four to six times per decade, and the decades themselves are the sample**; ⚠ **`#821`'s "highest-value single engineering change" is withdrawn here** — it looked obvious and two independent lines pointed at it, **and two lines pointing at the same thing is not evidence that the thing helps; they may simply be hitting the same wall**; this round cost one round, where rebuilding without measuring would have cost two **plus** a false foundation `[#822「那个「单点价值最高的改动」没用」]` (Entry 822) 〔GSS〕 ⟨positive control: in a **4× sample size** synthetic world both specifications' half-widths shrink clearly (0.504 / 0.460 against a 0.75 threshold) ⇒ this noise-measuring ruler does move; negative control: **the same specification run twice** returns a half-width ratio of **1.0091**, ⚠ **the reference is 1.0, not 0**, and **here the tolerance cannot be fixed in advance** — it compares two realisations of one quantity, so only that quantity can set the scale (twice its own noise half-width); ⚠ premise: **decades with exactly 3 years are excluded from the verdict** (`fitted` has 1 degree of freedom there); ⚠ **`G4`: both bootstraps run and both reported — reporting only `within` would have been self-congratulation, and that is exactly what happened**⟩|
| **The kill's yardstick was the wrong noise: seed spread measures reproducibility, not precision** | **a continuous estimand**, `concentration(item, axis)` = the single largest decade's |Δgap| ÷ the sum of all decades' |Δgap| (perfectly uniform ⇒ ≈1/number of decades; fully concentrated ⇒ ≈1.0; **the denominator is a sum of absolute values ≥ the numerator, so small denominators are structurally impossible — `#799`'s amplifier is designed out**), with the baseline **not assumed** but produced by a truly-uniform synthetic world for the same item and axis ⇒ absorbing unequal decade spans and partial coverage automatically; **the kill technically passed** (`homosex`'s REL−pol excess **+0.074**, same sign across 3 seeds, seed spread 0.016) — **while the negative control measured this statistic's uniform-world noise half-width on `homosex` at ±0.175** | ⚠⚠ **this round's real product**: **the yardstick I used, a seed spread of 0.016, is an order of magnitude smaller than the statistic's actual sampling noise of 0.175** ⇒ **seed spread measures whether the same data and the same code compute the same answer twice — it measures reproducibility, not precision; used as a noise scale it makes a difference sitting inside the noise look decisive**; ⚠ **and the error is hard to catch from the inside, because multi-seed is itself one of `realstat`'s hard requirements — I complied, reported the spread, and then treated that spread as something it is not** ⇒ **rule: the denominator in a pre-registered criterion must be the statistic's own sampling noise, never the consistency of repeated computation; the two can differ by an order of magnitude and both read as "stable"**; ⇒ **so `#820`'s "religion lurches, politics drifts" remains an untested post-hoc reading — this round neither rescued nor refuted it**; ⚠ **the positive control returns a capability boundary more durable than any conclusion: retention = 2/8** — `homosex` fires only narrowly (planted median 0.656 against its own uniform 97.5th percentile of 0.627), **while `teensex`, `sexeduc`, `suicide2`, `spanking`, `prayer` and `racmar` do not fire at all: their total movement is too small and noise swamps the concentration** ⇒ **an item that does not fire is not "unconcentrated" — it is an item this instrument can read nothing from**; ⚠⚠ **and two unrelated questions are now blocked by one design limit, which is itself the finding**: `#818` (noise floor 0.134 against a 0.10 target) and this round (0.175 against 0.074) **both die on "each decade uses only its first and last year"** ⇒ **using every available year within a decade is now the single highest-value engineering change in this project, and two independent lines point at it** `[#821「kill 的尺子用错了噪声」]` (Entry 821) 〔GSS, two axes inside one instrument〕 ⟨⚠ **the first version's controls had the wrong unit, and both failing at once was its fingerprint**: one planted/uniform draw per item, **with the spread across the 8 items read as the control's own sampling noise — that is between-item variation** ⇒ rebuilt at 40 repetitions per item; **and what that failure brought back — that the positive control only fires on large-movement items — is a real finding, not a bug that got fixed away**; negative control: tolerance 0.08 ÷ its own noise half-width 0.175 = **0.46×** ⇒ **the check declares itself unqualified to answer** (`#814`'s mechanism firing correctly for the second time on a question it was not built for); **3 seeds**, per-item spreads reported⟩|

**And `#821` had called "use every year within a decade" the highest-value single change — so it was measured before anything was rebuilt on it.** **The result: about a 15% reduction in the respondent-resampling sense; and in the year-resampling sense, none at all — slightly worse (ratio 1.090).** ⇒ **In the year-resampling sense, widening a decade from two years to five buys no precision — because year-to-year fluctuation is the dominant noise source and the years themselves are the sample. Using more of them is not "more data", it is that data.** ⇒ **What blocks those two lines is not the statistical specification but that GSS ran four to six times per decade — registered as structurally impossible.** ⇒ **A sentence about people, and this round it is about what can be known:** **how America's religious divides moved across fifty years, this data can tell us; which single decade was unusual, it cannot, and no amount of statistical work will change that — because the survey asked four to six times per decade, and the decades themselves are the sample.** ⚠ And "the highest-value single change" is withdrawn here: **two independent lines pointing at the same thing is not evidence that the thing helps — they may simply be hitting the same wall.** `[#822「那个「单点价值最高的改动」没用」]`
| **Sorted by devoutness the divide lurches; sorted by political conservatism the same divide merely drifts** | **the same `(item × decade)` grid run on both axes, with the comparison between the two grids as the estimand itself** (run as two separate rounds it could only be compared by eye): **under the religiosity axis the departures are — 1990s `homosex` −0.629 and `sexeduc` +0.055; 2000s `homosex` −0.476 and `teensex` −0.162; 2010s `teensex` −0.256**; **under the political axis the entire grid contains one departure (`sexeduc` 2010s −0.064), and `homosex` in the 1990s/2000s reaches only −0.293/−0.325, both unresolved** ⇒ **both pre-registered worlds died**: world B (the political axis is synchronized) fell to the kill (`polviews` modal decade holds **1 of 7**), and world A (the two axes give the same grid) does not hold either (cell agreement **30/39 = 76.9%**, and **8 of the 9 disagreements are "religiosity says departure, politics says unresolved"**) | ⚠⚠ **the pre-written confound was substantially refuted by measurement, which is what makes this round mean anything**: I had written "devoutness and political conservatism are highly correlated ⇒ the two top thirds may be the same people ⇒ agreement would prove nothing", **and the measured Jaccard overlap between the two top terciles has a median of 0.196, with a year-wise correlation median of +0.211** ⇒ **the two "top thirds" share only a fifth of their members; they are not the same people, so the contrast carries content**; ⇒ **a sentence about people: sort Americans by how religious they are and the divide over homosexuality lurches — it jumps in two particular decades. Sort the same Americans by how conservative they are and the same divide merely drifts, steadily, across fifty years. And these two sortings do not select the same people.**; ⚠⚠ **but "religion lurches, politics drifts" is read off the grid after the fact, not something this round pre-registered** — the three worlds I registered were "same grid", "political axis synchronized" and "unresolvable", **and the outcome is a fourth: neither axis is synchronized, but one jumps and one drifts** ⇒ **registered as the next round's pre-registration, not treated as a conclusion here**; ⚠⚠ **and this round exposes an instability in `#819` that has to be self-reported**: same data, same code, only `B` changed 1500→1200 with a different seed, and **`teensex` 2000s flipped from unmarked to `EXCLUDES` — 1 cell of 44 covered** ⇒ **`#819`'s "2/8" and this grid are both boundary-sensitive; `realstat` requires ≥3 seeds and these two rounds together supply 2 draws ⇒ until multi-seed is added, no single cell's marking may be cited as hard fact, and only the coarse conclusion "the mode is far below 6/8" is stable** `[#820「按虔诚分,鸿沟是猛地裂的」]` (Entry 820) 〔GSS — **a different axis inside one instrument, not a cross-instrument replication**〕 ⟨positive control: under both stratifications a synthetic world with all shift in the 1990s returns **1990s** as the mode; negative control: under both, the truly-uniform world yields **0 false positives** — ⚠ **written as `asserted`, not `identity_control`** (`#819`'s lesson); gauge: each cell's reference comes from that item's own full-span `Δgap` **under that stratification** ⇒ both item polarity and stratifier direction cancel; ⚠ **what could not be fixed**: `polviews` point estimates run systematically smaller than `REL`'s (`homosex` 1990s −0.293 against −0.629), **and "smaller" cannot be separated from "less resolvable" in this round** (the arms differ in both n and dispersion), **registered honestly**⟩|

**Whether `#820`'s post-hoc reading deserves belief depends on having a continuous, threshold-free quantity — so one was built:** **`concentration` = the single largest decade's movement ÷ the sum of all decades' movement, with the baseline supplied by a truly-uniform synthetic world.** **The kill technically passed (`homosex`'s difference between axes +0.074, same sign across three seeds, seed spread 0.016) — and the negative control measured this statistic's own sampling noise half-width at ±0.175.** ⇒ **The yardstick I had used was an order of magnitude smaller than the statistic's actual noise.** **Seed spread measures whether the same data and the same code compute the same answer twice — it measures reproducibility, not precision.** **Used as a noise scale, it makes a difference sitting inside the noise look decisive.** ⚠ And the error is hard to catch from the inside, **because multi-seed is itself a hard requirement — I complied, reported the spread, and then treated it as something it is not.** ⇒ So "religion lurches, politics drifts" **remains an untested post-hoc reading**; this round neither rescued nor refuted it. ⚠⚠ And two unrelated questions are now blocked by one design limit: `#818` and this round **both die on "each decade uses only its first and last year"** — **that convergence is itself the finding, and it names the single highest-value engineering change in this project.** `[#821「kill 的尺子用错了噪声」]`

measured, it is **not** ⇒ **a sentence about people: on seven of the eight questions the distance between the devout and everyone else moved at its own steady pace across fifty years, with no decade standing out; ⚠ **the sentence I can no longer write: "the 1990s–2000s are when this religious divide opened"** — without the qualifier "on homosexuality", it is false ` ⚠⚠ **appended by `#828`**: the **`sexeduc`/1990s and `teensex`/2010s cells named in this row do not survive `#826`'s BH multiplicity correction** (p = 0.034 and 0.0067; negative control: each item genuinely uniform ⇒ **0 false positives across 44 covered cells**; ⚠ the pre-written confound: **the eight items have different coverage ⇒ decades never asked are marked `NOT_ASKED` and removed from the denominator (4 cells), never scored as "did not open" — inflating the denominator would manufacture a modal decade by itself**; ⚠⚠ **the negative control was this family's fourth repeat** (`#818` established the rule one round earlier): the measured false-positive count is 0 ⇒ `identity_control(0.0, 0.0)` has both sides exactly zero ⇒ the library flagged it DEGENERATE, **and the library was right** — **but this zero was measured, not algebraically forced, so it is not a check that should not have been made but a check that cannot be written as an equality; and it is admissible only because the positive control has shown the instrument can fire (`P5★`: a zero from an instrument that has never returned non-zero is silence, not acquittal)**; ⚠ the intervals are **lower bounds** (each decade uses only its first and last year) · `matters = 0.10` **is my choice** · the 2020s have too few years ⇒ **"anything after the twenty-tens" is unanswerable here**⟩ ⚠⚠ **appended by `#824` — the "2.5×" in this row is withdrawn**: that ratio was computed in **raw scale points** across items with 2–5 categories; within the 34-cell family they are precisely the ~1.7 cells `#825` computed as luck's share) ⇒ **they are no longer departures; only `homosex`'s 1990s/2000s survive, and under BY only the 1990s.**|

**And `#819`'s sentence rested on a premise I had never tested: it was stratified by devoutness. So the same grid was re-run on political views, with the comparison between the two grids as the estimand itself.** **The result: under religiosity, `homosex` departs by −0.629 in the 1990s and −0.476 in the 2000s; under political views the same item in the same decades reaches only −0.293 and −0.325, and both are unresolved. The entire political grid contains one departure.** ⇒ **A sentence about people: sort Americans by how religious they are and the divide over homosexuality lurches — it jumps in two particular decades. Sort the same Americans by how conservative they are and the same divide merely drifts, steadily, across fifty years.** **And these two sortings do not select the same people: the two "top thirds" share only a fifth of their members (Jaccard 0.196) — that was the strongest confound I wrote down before running, and the measurement refuted it, which is exactly what makes this round mean anything.** ⚠⚠ But **"religion lurches, politics drifts" is read off the grid after the fact**, not pre-registered here — it is registered as the next round's target. ⚠⚠ And this round also exposes an instability in `#819`: **changing only the seed and `B` flipped 1 of 44 covered cells** — **until multi-seed is added, no single cell may be cited as hard fact.** `[#820「按虔诚分,鸿沟是猛地裂的」]`
| **UNVERIFIED — and the reason is that this design's noise floor is larger than the amount worth talking about** | **the first single-stratum estimand in this line** (every number since `#802` has been a difference between strata, **and a difference can move while nobody does, and hold still while both groups move**): the devout stratum's own within-cohort attitude change, **2000s = −0.0759 [−0.2309, +0.0764] ⇒ `UNRESOLVED`, compatible with {−0.10, 0.0, +0.10}**; against **1990s = +0.1663 [+0.0327, +0.2972] ⇒ `EXCLUDES`** | ⚠⚠ **the reason for the UNVERIFIED is this round's actual product, not a failure to produce one**: the negative control's own measured noise half-width is **0.134**, while the pre-run declaration of what magnitude matters was **0.10** ⇒ **the design's noise floor exceeds the quantity it must resolve ⇒ "stopped" and "turned back" are structurally inseparable here** ⇒ **registered as a capability boundary, not a debt**; **and this is the first time `#815`'s mechanism fired correctly on a question it was not built for**: with the tolerance fixed at 0.05 in advance (`#817`②'s repair) the ratio is **0.37×** ⇒ **the check declares itself unqualified to answer, and the correct response is not to loosen the tolerance, which would be bending the gate to fit the result**; ⚠⚠ **but one thing was resolved, and it does not depend on the failed control**: **the 2000s interval excludes the 1990s value of +0.166** ⇒ **what the devout did in the two-thousands is not what they did in the nineties — in the nineties they were growing more permissive; in the two-thousands they were no longer doing that. Whether they stopped or reversed cannot be answered here** (this comparison is between two observed intervals, not between a quantity and zero) `[#818「UNVERIFIED,而理由是这个设计的噪声底」]` (Entry 818) 〔GSS〕 ⟨⚠⚠ **all three control errors were mine, all three were caught by the gate, and two of them are repeats within the same family**: ① the first positive control **added −0.300 on top of the real data**, which already contains +0.166 ⇒ the expected recovery was −0.134 and −0.1368 came back — **the instrument was right and my expected value was wrong; this is the third repeat of `#812`'s "destroy the structure under test", which `#812` had already written into the ledger, and the lesson did not transfer**; ② the first negative control froze attitudes within cohorts, making the term **algebraically exactly 0** ⇒ comparing 0 to 0, which the library flagged DEGENERATE — **and the library was right** (`#802`'s added premise to `#770` is that the two values must be non-zero) ⇒ **the check I actually wanted was never "are these two quantities the same" but "is this quantity exactly zero", and a quantity that is identically zero can only support an implementation check, never a control** ⇒ **so the negative control was split in two: an algebraic implementation check (deterministic) and a genuinely stochastic one (y1 resampled from its own cohort's y0 distribution) — only the latter measures noise, and only the latter can fail**; ⚠ the pre-registered confound: the devout stratum is defined by within-year terciles, so the two years are not the same people; the stratum's own `REL` mean drifts +0.852→+0.843 (1990s) and +0.834→+0.841 (2000s), **small, but this design cannot fix it, only measure it**⟩|

**And `#812`'s "it split open in the nineties" had only ever been run on one item, with `#813`/`#816`/`#817`/`#818` all stacked on top of it — so this round asked what none of those four had: did the eight items open in the same decade?** **This is a meta-separator, and its positive outcome was one I did not want: had the timing been shared, this divide would not be about what a society does with sexual difference but about when religiosity became a political identity.** **Measured, it is not. The modal decade holds only 2 of 8 items.** ⇒ **A sentence about people: on seven of the eight questions the distance between the devout and everyone else moved at its own steady pace across fifty years, with no decade standing out; only homosexuality lurched, and its two lurching decades are 2.5× the largest cell anywhere else in the grid.** **This divide is not one instance of a general religious-political realignment — within its own family it is the exception.** ⚠⚠ **And `helpblk` overturns how I had been reading `#806`**: its gap crossed zero **while no decade of it departs from its own uniform rate** — **a divide can travel all the way to the other side without ever lurching once. "Changing sides" and "splitting open" are two different things, and I had been treating them as two phrasings of one.** `[#819「只有同性恋那一题在猛地裂」]`
| **Neither opening was pure "movement" — and in the two-thousands the devout did not move at all** | **the cohort decomposition extended to the 2000s** (`#813` called that decade "almost entirely divergence", **and it had not tested the "movement" premise either**): 1990s Δgap −0.587 = attitude −0.501 + composition −0.086 ⇒ share **14.7% [1.1%, 37.9%]**; 2000s Δgap −0.466 = attitude −0.423 + composition −0.043 ⇒ share **9.2% [0.5%, 37.8%]** ⇒ **the intervals overlap, so the decades' mechanisms cannot be told apart; and both share intervals exclude 0, so neither opening was pure "movement"** | ⚠⚠ **placing them side by side surfaced something neither `#813` nor `#816` had seen, and it matters more than the shares: what the devout stratum did differs completely between the two decades** — **in the nineties the devout's attitude term is +0.166 (they were growing more permissive too, just slowly); in the two-thousands it is −0.063 (not slower — slightly backwards)**, against +0.667 → +0.360 for everyone else ⇒ **a sentence about people: in the nineties Americans were all moving the same way and the devout were merely slower; by the two-thousands everyone else kept going while the devout stopped, and edged back. The divide widened by a similar amount in both decades, and they are not the same event**; ⚠ **and I have given −0.063 no interval** — it was read off a decomposition, **not a quantity this round pre-registered** ⇒ **registered as next round's target, not treated as a conclusion here**; ⚠⚠ **and the negative control's row printed "tolerance ÷ its own noise half-width = 1.00×", a number that exposes a self-defeat in the mechanism `#815` built**: I wrote the tolerance as `max(noise half-width, 1e-3)` ⇒ **the ratio is identically 1.00× by construction and that check can never fire** ⇒ **`#815`'s rule is "the tolerance may not be narrower than the noise", and a caller who simply sets `tolerance := noise` satisfies it forever while constraining nothing — a check-that-cannot-fail reappearing inside a mechanism built to prevent checks-that-fire-at-random. I dug the same hole while filling it**; ⚠ **this does not invalidate this round's negative control** (it still required the share to land within that width of 0, and it did); **what is invalidated is the extra assurance the ratio claimed** `[#817「两次张开都不是纯粹的「走」」]` (Entry 817) 〔GSS〕 ⟨positive control (attitudes frozen) ⇒ share **identically 1.000000**, and **declared `deterministic=True`** per `#816`② — **this time the row says what it actually is**; negative control (weights frozen) ⇒ median **0.039** [0.002, 0.209], ⚠ **here the reference really is 0**; both decades' cohort-weight movement printed for both strata (1990s 0.174/0.177, 2000s 0.140/0.181) — ⚠ **this design still cannot separate "composition within a stratum changed" from "the stratum's definition drifted"**; ⚠ **the decomposition is an identity, not evidence** (`realstat`'s arithmetic trap)⟩|
| **Mostly people changed their minds — but about one seventh is not "movement" at all, it is who is in the room** | **the 1990→1998 `Δgap` decomposed by birth cohort** (1910s–1970s, 7 bins): devout +0.215 = attitude **+0.166** + composition +0.049; non-devout +0.802 = attitude **+0.667** + composition +0.135 ⇒ **`Δgap` −0.587 = attitude −0.501 + composition −0.086, a composition share of 14.7% with a bootstrap interval of [1.2%, 36.5%] — the upper bound crosses the pre-registered 1/3, so the kill fired against expectation** | ⚠⚠ **`#813`'s phrase "two groups moving in different directions" presupposed that the same people moved, and that premise had never been tested** — **in a repeated cross-section a change in means can require nobody to have changed their mind, provided the young cohorts entering differ from the old ones leaving; ② **this design cannot separate "the composition within a stratum changed" from "the stratum's definition drifted"** (strata are within-year terciles) ⇒ written before the run and registered after: **the two strata's total cohort-weight movement is 0.174 and 0.177, which is not small**; ⚠⚠ **and the decomposition is an identity, stated before running**: attitude + composition ≡ total, true by construction ⇒ **the decomposition is not evidence, it is algebra** (`realstat`'s arithmetic trap) — **only the relative size of the two terms and their stability under resampling carry evidential weight** `[#816「大部分是改了主意」]` (Entry 816) 〔GSS〕 ⟨negative control (cohort weights frozen, only attitudes moving) ⇒ share median **0.044** [0.003, 0.237], ⚠ **here the reference really is 0**, and the tolerance is taken from its **own measured noise half-width of 0.117**, per the rule `#815` had just established; **And placing the two decades side by side surfaced something the previous two rounds had not seen, and it matters more than either share: what the devout stratum did differs completely between them.** **In the nineties the devout's attitude term is +0.166 — they were growing more permissive too, just slowly. In the two-thousands it is −0.063 — not slower, but slightly backwards.** (Everyone else over the same decades: +0.667 → +0.360.) ⇒ **A sentence about people: in the nineties Americans were all moving the same way and the devout were merely slower; by the two-thousands everyone else kept going while the devout stopped, and edged back.** **The divide widened by a similar amount in both decades, and they are not the same event.** ⚠ And **−0.063 has no interval** — it was read off a decomposition rather than pre-registered as a target, **so it is registered for the next round and not treated as a conclusion here.** `[#817「两次张开都不是纯粹的「走」」]`

`deterministic=True` marks a deterministic quantity where noise does not apply; **supplying neither leaves a visible `⚠ noise unmeasured` marker on the row**; **backtested on 4 cases with known answers ⇒ 4/4** (`#805`'s control at 0.08/0.35 ⇒ **caught, printing 0.23×**; the third is the most dangerous, because it fails sometimes and so every pass reads like a validation** ⇒ **judging a check cannot stop at "can it fail" — it has to ask "when it fails, is it because the thing under test was wrong"**; ⚠⚠ **and "mark, don't block" is a reasoned choice here rather than softness**: every `identity_control` already run in this ledger is a re-runnable artifact, and **blocking would use a new rule to destroy old evidence** (`L81`: annotate, never rewrite) ⇒ **new code supplies the parameter, old code carries the marker, both are auditable, and nothing is waved through silently**; ⚠ **what could not be fixed**: `noise_half_width` still has to be measured by the caller — **the library does not know how the quantity under test was computed and cannot redraw it 200 times on their behalf** ⇒ **the rule turns "measure the noise" from something nobody remembers into something that leaves a scar if skipped, but it cannot do the measuring** `[#815「恒真、恒假、随机」]` (Entry 815) 〔this page's own gate library〕 ⟨the same road as `#795`/`#804`/`#811`, for the **fourth** time: a rule written only in the ledger fails; | **Both openings were genuine divergence — and `#805`/`#806` drew their negative control once, so those passes were luck** | **the common-shift control run on the two decades where something actually happened** (`matters` = 0.25, supplied explicitly per `#811`): **1990s +0.272 [+0.068,+0.737] and +0.370 [+0.091,+0.986], both EXCLUDING 1**; **2000s +0.008 [−0.128,+0.266] and +0.010 [−0.173,+0.348], both EXCLUDING 1**; the control decade, the 1980s, returns four intervals spanning ±7 to ±14, **all UNRESOLVED** ⇒ **both openings were two groups genuinely moving in different directions: in the nineties roughly seven-tenths is not explicable by the scale, and in the two-thousands almost none of it is** | ⚠⚠ **`#805`'s published [+0.240, +0.956] is narrowed in meaning by this, though its number is not wrong**: it was computed over the whole fifty years, which `#812` has shown to be heterogeneous ⇒ **it blends two decades that split open with three that did not move, so it describes no process that actually occurred** ⇒ **the general lesson: a decomposition spanning a heterogeneous period decomposes that period's average, not what happened inside it — `#805`'s number is right, and it answers a question nobody asked**; ⚠⚠⚠ **and the most transferable finding lands on `#805` and `#806` themselves**: the first version of the negative control drew once, returned +0.8475 against a tolerance of 0.10 ⇒ FAIL, **and loosening the tolerance would be bending the gate to fit the result, which is not allowed** ⇒ redrawn **200 times: median +1.0057, 95% range [+0.7657, +1.4390]** ⇒ **the instrument is unbiased and that failure was sampling noise**; **but looking back, `#805` and `#806` drew the same negative control once each, against a tolerance of 0.08 — four times tighter than the control's own sampling spread of ±0.33** ⇒ **a control whose tolerance sits inside its own noise floor fires at random: passing establishes nothing and failing establishes nothing. `#805`'s +0.9917 and `#806`'s +1.0473 both sit inside that range, so those passes were luck, not validation** — ⚠ **this does not overturn either round's conclusion;
